Extensive experiments on standard ICS benchmarks demonstrate that CG-ICS not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also substantially improves robustness, yielding a more reliable ICS system with significantly reduced variance across diverse reference choices.

</details>


### [72] [HAT-4D: Lifting Monocular Video for 4D Multi-Object Interactions via Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28215)
*Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Wu,Zhenkai Zhang,Xinrui Shi,Haoyuan Wang,Yichen Zhao,Su Linxiang,Chenyang Yu,Mingyu Zhang,Yifan Ding,Boran Wen,Li Zhang,Ruiyang Liu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAT-4D 是一个面向单目视频中多物体动态交互重建的智能体框架，结合视觉语言模型与分层人在回路反馈，在复杂遮挡和深度歧义下生成更物理合理、时间一致的 4D 交互资产，并构建了相应基准 MVOIK-4D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目 4D 重建方法多关注孤立物体，难以处理真实开放场景中多物体交互带来的严重遮挡、复杂动态和深度歧义；而高效从海量野外单目视频中提取 4D 交互数据，对扩展具身智能数据和训练视觉-语言-动作模型具有重要价值。

Method: 提出 HAT-4D，融合 VLM 与多层级 human-in-the-loop 反馈机制，在 3D 生成和 4D 时序传播过程中修正深度歧义、遮挡和交互关系；同时构建 MVOIK-4D 开放世界单目 4D 交互重建基准，并设计关注物理合理性与时间一致性的多维评价协议。

Result: 实验显示 HAT-4D 在多数评价指标上达到 SOTA，并保持有竞争力的语义对齐能力；消融实验表明少量人工反馈能显著改善交互重建；由 HAT-4D 生成的数据用于微调时可提升基线模型表现。

Conclusion: HAT-4D 为从单目视频中规模化获取多物体 4D 交互数据提供了有效方案，在物理可信、多对象交互和时间一致性方面优于现有方法，并具备作为数据引擎支撑具身 AI/VLA 训练的潜力。

Abstract: Extracting dynamic 4D object interactions from massive, in-the-wild monocular videos offers a highly efficient data collection pathway for scaling Embodied AI and training VLAs. However, existing monocular 4D reconstruction methods primarily focus on isolated objects, often failing under the severe occlusions and complex dynamics inherent in multi-object interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose HAT-4D, the first agentic framework designed to reconstruct the 3D geometry, temporal dynamics, and physical interactions of multiple objects from a single video. By integrating VLMs with a multi-level human-in-the-loop feedback mechanism, HAT-4D efficiently resolves depth ambiguities and interaction-induced occlusions during 3D generation and 4D propagation, yielding physically plausible assets without relying on expensive multicamera rigs. As a scalable data engine, HAT-4D facilitates the creation of MVOIK-4D, an open-world benchmark for monocular 4D interaction reconstruction, accompanied by a novel multi-dimensional evaluation protocol focused on physical plausibility and temporal consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-4D achieves SOTA performance on most evaluation metrics, while maintaining competitive semantic alignment. Ablation studies show that introducing a small amount of human feedback improves interaction reconstruction. Moreover, the data produced by HAT-4D effectively improves baseline performance when used for fine-tuning. Our data and code are available at https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/

</details>


### [73] [Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning with Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27988)
*Xirui Teng,Nan Xi,Junsong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LVDR，用关键点引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索建模细粒度技能评估中的潜在视觉推理过程，在保证评估性能的同时提供可解释的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估方法虽能对运动、手术等细粒度技能表现进行评分，但多为黑箱模型，难以显式展示模型如何通过逐步视觉推理得出最终判断，限制了可解释性与可信度。

Method: 提出 Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning（LVDR）框架，将关键点引导的 Monte Carlo Tree Search（MCTS）融入潜在视觉推理过程建模；通过搜索和可视化关键视觉线索序列，揭示哪些动作片段或姿态变化对最终质量评估起关键作用。

Result: 在覆盖多类体育与手术场景的四个数据集上进行实验，LVDR 获得了有竞争力的定量评估表现，并能输出通向最终预测的可解释视觉推理轨迹。

Conclusion: LVDR 在动作质量评估中兼顾准确性与可解释性，为细粒度技能分析提供了从视觉证据到最终评分的可视化推理路径，提升了模型判断的透明度。

Abstract: Analyzing fine-grained skill activities (e.g., sports, surgery) requires not only recognizing visual patterns but also performing step-by-step visual reasoning that leads to the final judgment. While recent advances in action quality assessment have achieved remarkable progress in evaluating performance, existing models remain black boxes, where they lack the ability to explicitly reveal the reasoning processes underlying their judgments. To address this limitation, we propose Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning (LVDR), a novel framework that integrates keypoint-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to model and visualize the latent visual reasoning process. LVDR not only produces more accurate skill assessments but also uncovers the critical visual reasoning sequences that contribute to the final evaluation. Extensive experiments across four datasets spanning diverse sports and surgical domains demonstrate that LVDR achieves competitive quantitative performance while providing interpretable visual reasoning trajectories leading to the final predictions. Source codes and models can be found through the following link: https://github.com/XiruiTeng/LVDR_Official.git.

</details>


### [74] [Exposure Bias Can Alleviate Itself via Directional and Frequency Rectification in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28226)
*Guanbo Huang,Jingjia Mao,Fanding Huang,Fengkai Liu,Xiangyang Luo,Yaoyuan Liang,Jiasheng Lu,Xiaoe Wang,Pei Liu,Ruiliu Fu,Ruqi Huang,Shao-Lun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DEFAR 框架，通过在训练中模拟单步推理来显式识别 Flow Matching 的曝光偏差，并利用偏差自身的方向与频率信息进行自适应校正，从而提升生成质量与推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 在生成任务中表现优秀，但训练与推理过程不一致会导致曝光偏差；现有方法多依赖静态约束或外部启发式，难以动态适应推理时累积的偏移。因此作者希望直接利用曝光偏差中蕴含的动态反馈信号来进行自我纠偏。

Method: 提出 DirEctional-Frequency Adaptive Rectification（DEFAR）。该方法在训练阶段模拟单步推理以暴露偏差，并包含两个模块：Anti-Drift Rectification（ADR）将推理漂移视为方向信号，学习把偏离状态拉回目标轨迹；Frequency Compensation（FC）观察到高噪声阶段低频成分不足会导致偏差累积，因此用偏差本身作为自反馈权重来增强缺失的低频成分。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CelebA-64、ImageNet-256/512 等数据集上的实验显示，DEFAR 优于已有基线，并体现出较好的可扩展性、与其他方法的兼容性以及推理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DEFAR 表明曝光偏差不仅是需要抑制的问题，也可以作为动态自反馈信号来指导模型校正；通过方向纠偏与频率补偿，模型获得更强的偏差容忍与自修复能力。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) has achieved remarkable generative performance, yet it suffers from exposure bias due to discrepancies between training and inference. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on static constraints or external heuristics. In this work, we propose that exposure bias itself inherently contains dynamic signals that can guide its own rectification. To leverage this, we introduce DEFAR (DirEctional-Frequency Adaptive Rectification). This framework simulates the single-step inference process during training to identify exposure bias. It utilizes directional and frequency-adaptive feedback signals from the bias itself to enhance the model's bias tolerance. It consists of two key components: (1) Anti-Drift Rectification (ADR). ADR treats inference-time drift as a signal to learn the direction to steer deviated states back toward the target. ADR endows the model with intrinsic active self-rectification capabilities; (2) Frequency Compensation (FC). Empirically, we observe that accumulated bias often stems from a lack of low-frequency components in high-noise stages, and exposure bias carries the missing frequency. FC leverages the bias itself as a self-feedback weighting factor to reinforce the missing frequency components. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CelebA-64, and ImageNet-256/512 show that DEFAR outperforms prior baselines and further demonstrates favorable scalability, compatibility, and inference robustness.

</details>


### [75] [HumanMoveVQA: Can Video MLLMs reason about human movement in videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27999)
*Pulkit Gera,Faegheh Sardari,Asmar Nadeem,Valentina Bono,Padraig Boulton,Adrian Hilton,Armin Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HumanMoveVQA 是一个面向视频中人体全局轨迹与朝向推理的新基准，用世界一致的三维运动监督揭示并改善现有 MLLM 在深层人体运动理解上的能力短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型虽在高层视频理解上进展迅速，但往往将复杂人体运动简化为粗粒度语义标签；现有基准偏重场景事件或局部关节动作，缺乏对人在空间中随时间发生的全局位移、轨迹和朝向变化的系统评测。

Method: 提出 HumanMoveVQA 基准，采用以首帧为锚点的世界坐标系，保留相对于固定起点的平移与旋转信息；设计多阶段可扩展流程，将二维视频观测提升为世界一致的三维运动轨迹，并生成超过 1 万个结构化问答样本，覆盖七类推理任务，包括运动聚合、时序排序和轨迹级推理等。

Result: 对先进闭源模型的广泛评测显示，它们在深层人体运动理解方面存在显著能力缺口；同时，通过使用该基准提供的目标化、世界一致监督对开源基线进行微调，可获得明显性能提升。

Conclusion: HumanMoveVQA 为评估和训练具备运动感知能力的视频理解模型提供了严格的几何基础，表明全局人体运动推理是当前 MLLM 的关键短板，但也是可通过针对性监督学习改进的问题。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advance of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in high-level video understanding, a fundamental bottleneck remains: these models collapse complex human motion into coarse semantic labels. Existing benchmarks mostly focus on scene-centric events or local joint articulations, failing to probe global human motion in space over time (trajectory and orientation changes). We introduce HumanMoveVQA, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate global trajectory and orientation reasoning from an exocentric perspective. Our benchmark utilizes a first-frame anchored world coordinate system, preserving translation and rotation relative to a fixed starting point. We propose a scalable, multi-stage pipeline that lifts 2D video observations into world-consistent 3D motion tracks to generate over 10K structured question-answer pairs across seven reasoning categories, including motion aggregation, sequential ordering, and trajectory-level inference. Our extensive evaluation reveals a critical capability gap in state-of-the-art proprietary models on deep human motion understanding. However, we demonstrate that this is a learnable problem; by fine-tuning an open-source baseline with our targeted, world-consistent supervision, we achieve a significant improvement.HumanMoveVQA establishes a rigorous geometric foundation for developing next-generation, movement-aware video understanding models.

</details>


### [76] [Learning Topology-Aware Representations via Test-Time Adaptation for Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28268)
*Ali Zia,Usman Ali,Abdul Rehman,Umer Ramzan,Kang Han,Muhammad Faheem,Shahnawaz Qureshi,Wei Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoTTA 将持久同调引入异常分割的测试时自适应，通过拓扑伪标签约束结构一致性，在多个 2D/3D 基准上显著提升 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常分割 TTA 依赖置信度阈值、熵最小化等像素级启发式方法，难以在噪声和纹理变化下保持结构一致性；同时将异常图视为平坦强度场，忽略复杂缺陷几何中的高阶空间关系。

Method: 提出 TopoTTA：对异常分数图进行多层 cubical complex filtration，利用持久同调提取稳健的拓扑伪标签，并用其指导轻量级测试时分类器进行适应；不重训练骨干网络，也减少对原始分数阈值二值化的依赖。

Result: 在 MVTec AD、VisA、Real-IAD、MVTec 3D-AD、AnomalyShapeNet、MVTec LOCO 六个基准上，相比先进无监督异常检测与分割方法平均 F1 提升 15%，尤其在具有复杂几何或结构变化的异常上收益最大。

Conclusion: 将拓扑推理融入测试时自适应可提供结构感知的泛化机制，提升异常分割在分布偏移、噪声和复杂形状下的鲁棒性，连接几何学习与鲁棒自适应。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a promising paradigm for mitigating distribution shifts in deep models. However, existing TTA approaches for anomaly segmentation remain limited by their reliance on pixel-level heuristics, such as confidence thresholding or entropy minimisation, which fail to preserve structural consistency under noise and texture variation. Moreover, they typically treat anomaly maps as flat intensity fields, ignoring the higher-order spatial relationships that characterise complex defect geometries. We introduce TopoTTA (Topological Test-Time Adaptation), a novel framework that integrates persistent homology, a tool from topological data analysis, into the TTA pipeline to enforce geometric and structural coherence during adaptation. By applying multi-level cubical complex filtration to anomaly score maps, TopoTTA derives robust topological pseudo-labels that guide a lightweight test-time classifier, enhancing segmentation quality without retraining the backbone model. The approach avoids reliance on method-specific raw-score thresholding for mask binarisation, preserves connectivity, and generalises across both 2D and 3D modalities. Extensive experiments across six standard benchmarks (MVTec AD, VisA, Real-IAD, MVTec 3D-AD, AnomalyShapeNet, and MVTec LOCO) demonstrate an average 15% F1 improvement over state-of-the-art unsupervised anomaly detection and segmentation methods, with the largest gains on anomalies exhibiting complex geometric or structural variations. These findings suggest that integrating topological reasoning into test-time adaptation provides a principled route to structure-aware generalisation, bridging the gap between geometric learning and robust adaptation.

</details>


### [77] [Curriculum-guided Change Detection Training: Toward Accurate Serac Fall Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28012)
*Arthur Dérédel,Carlos Crispim-Junior,Pierre Lemaire,Johan Berthet,Laure Tougne Rodet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向变化检测的课程学习训练框架，通过按样本难度逐步引入训练数据，相比均匀采样可提升模型鲁棒性与检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度变化检测方法多依赖均匀采样，默认所有样本对优化贡献相同；但实际多时相图像受采集条件和外观差异影响，均匀采样可能带来噪声梯度，削弱鲁棒表征学习。因此需要探索替代训练范式。

Method: 作者设计课程学习框架，使用两类互补难度度量：Solar Angular Gap（SAG）作为基于物理采集条件差异的难度代理，SSIM衡量图像对外观结构相似性。训练过程中从较容易样本开始，逐步引入更具挑战性的样本，实现由粗到细的表征学习。

Result: 在SeracFallDet基准上，该方法相较标准均匀采样策略在像素级和目标级变化检测方法上均取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 课程学习可有效改善深度变化检测的训练鲁棒性；该框架与具体变化检测架构正交，因而可广泛迁移到多种现有方法中。

Abstract: Change Detection (CD) aims to identify semantic or structural changes from nearly registered multi-temporal images. While recent advances in training methodologies have largely focused on semi-supervised learning and consistency regularization, alternative training paradigms remain underexplored. In particular, most deep CD methods rely on uniform sampling during training, implicitly assuming that all training samples contribute equally to the optimization process. However, such naive sampling can introduce noisy gradients and hinder robust representation learning. To address this limitation, we propose a curriculum learning framework tailored for change detection. Our approach investigates two complementary difficulty measures: the Solar Angular Gap (SAG), a physically grounded proxy for acquisition-condition variability, and the Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM), which evaluates appearance similarity between image pairs. Based on these criteria, the framework progressively introduces challenging samples during training, enabling models to learn robust representations in a coarse-to-fine manner. We evaluate our method on the challenging SeracFallDet benchmark, where results demonstrate consistent improvements of the proposed approach over standard uniform-sampling strategies for both pixel-based and object-based approaches. These results highlight the potential of curriculum learning to improve robustness in deep change detection. Importantly, our training framework is orthogonal to existing CD architectures, making it readily applicable to a broad range of methods.

</details>


### [78] [TempAct: Advancing Temporal Plausibility in Autoregressive Video Generation via Planner-Executor RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28016)
*Jing Wang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jiajun Liang,Kaiqi Liu,Wanyun Pang,Zhenyu Xie,Tianyu Pang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TempAct 是一种用于自回归视频扩散模型的规划器-执行器强化学习框架，旨在改善分块生成中的时序指令遵循与动作顺序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型按片段生成视频，虽支持低延迟流式生成，但全局提示难以明确每个片段应发生的子事件；逐步切换提示又容易导致反应滞后、语义混合和跨提示转移的误差传播。传统 SFT 存在曝光偏差，蒸馏方法也未直接优化动作顺序和提示转换正确性。

Method: 提出 TempAct 框架：使用 LLM 规划器探索带时间跨度的可执行步骤提示，并训练自回归扩散执行器在自身生成历史条件下执行这些提示。核心机制是层次化组探索：候选计划构成规划组，每个计划从共享视觉上下文产生多个执行延续，从而实现计划级和执行级信用分配。奖励设计包括计划质量、整段视频时序反馈、局部转换级步骤遵循奖励、美学正则化以及 KL 约束。

Result: 在 Self-Forcing 和 LongLive 上的实验表明，TempAct 能提升视频生成的时间一致性，同时保持整体视觉质量。

Conclusion: TempAct 通过联合优化时序分解与步骤条件执行，有效缓解自回归视频扩散模型在分块生成中的提示歧义和转场错误问题，是面向长时序、低延迟视频生成的一种有效强化学习方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models enable low-latency streaming generation by synthesizing videos chunk by chunk with cached visual context, but this chunk-wise formulation makes temporal instruction following ambiguous. A single global prompt does not specify which sub-event should be realized in each chunk, while naively switching to step-wise prompts often leads to delayed reactions, blended step semantics, and error propagation across prompt transitions. These failures are difficult to address with supervised fine-tuning or distillation alone: SFT suffers from exposure bias, while rollout-based distillation still optimizes low-level denoising or teacher-distribution matching rather than directly enforcing action ordering and prompt-transition correctness. We address these challenges with TempAct, a planner--executor reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes temporal decomposition and step-conditioned execution for temporally plausible AR video generation. TempAct uses an LLM planner to explore span-aware step prompts that are executable by the video model, and trains an AR diffusion executor to follow these prompts under its own generated histories. Its key mechanism is hierarchical group exploration: candidate plans form planning groups, and each plan induces an execution group of multiple continuations from a shared visual context, enabling plan-level credit assignment for long-horizon temporal outcomes and executor-level credit assignment for prompt-switch behavior. We further design hierarchical rewards that combine plan-quality and full-video temporal feedback for the planner with local transition-level step-following rewards, aesthetic regularization, and KL constraints for the executor. Experiments on Self-Forcing and LongLive show that TempAct improves temporal consistency while preserving overall visual quality.

</details>


### [79] [EMOSH: Expressive Motion and Shape Disentanglement for Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28026)
*Dongbin Zhang,Hao Liu,Binquan Dai,Kangjie Chen,Chuming Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EMOSH 提出以 Expressive Human Model（EHM）为核心控制表示的人体视频生成框架，在保持身份与体型解耦的同时提升表情和手势驱动的表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控人体动画方法在表现力与解耦性之间存在矛盾：2D 姿态条件方法容易产生运动-体型纠缠，导致驱动人物体型泄漏；基于 3D 先验的方法虽能较好解耦几何形状，但难以表达面部表情和复杂手势，动画效果偏僵硬。

Method: 论文引入 Expressive Human Model（EHM）作为控制表示，通过显式解耦 shape 与 pose 参数解决体型泄漏问题，并设计鲁棒运动跟踪器从视频中估计 EHM 参数；同时提出 Coarse-to-Fine Hybrid Motion Injection 策略以实现更细粒度的表情和手势控制，并采用 Spatially-Aligned Conditioning 机制缓解训练与推理阶段的域差异、提升身份一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，EMOSH 在 self-driven 和 cross-driven 场景中均优于既有方法，能够生成高保真、表情生动且体型解耦良好的人体视频。

Conclusion: EMOSH 通过表达性人体模型、混合运动注入和空间对齐条件机制，在人体动画生成中较好兼顾了可控性、表现力、身份一致性与体型解耦，为数字人和内容创作提供了更可靠的生成框架。

Abstract: High-fidelity and expressive controllable human animation is essential for content creation and digital avatar applications. However, existing methods face a dilemma between expressiveness and disentanglement. Mainstream 2D pose-conditioned approaches suffer from "motion-shape entanglement", leading to the leakage of the driving subject's body shape. Conversely, methods relying on 3D priors (e.g., SMPL) achieve geometric disentanglement but struggle to capture facial expressions and complex gestures, resulting in rigid animations. To this end, we propose EMOSH, a novel framework for high-fidelity controllable human video generation. First, an Expressive Human Model (EHM) is introduced as the core control representation. By explicitly disentangling shape and pose parameters, we fundamentally resolve the body shape leakage issue. Alongside this, a robust motion tracker is designed to accurately estimate EHM parameters from video. Second, we propose a Coarse-to-Fine Hybrid Motion Injection strategy, enabling more fine-grained control over expressions and gestures. Furthermore, we introduce a Spatially-Aligned Conditioning mechanism to bridge the domain gap between training and inference, improving identity consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMOSH outperforms previous methods in both self-driven and cross-driven scenarios, producing high-fidelity videos with vivid expressions while maintaining shape disentanglement.

</details>


### [80] [AirGroundBench: Probing Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal Large Models under Heterogeneous Multi-View Embodied Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28049)
*Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Leyuan Wang,Jinshan Lai,Keyang Wang,Zonghao Guo,Qiang Ma,Liuyu Xiang,Jianwei Hu,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AirGroundBench 是一个面向 UAV-UGV 协作场景的多视角空间智能诊断基准，用于评估 MLLM 在跨视角几何一致理解与具身决策中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在具身智能中展现潜力，但其在异构视角之间保持几何一致空间理解的能力缺乏系统评估；现有基准多关注单智能体、单视角感知，难以覆盖空地协作中的尺度不匹配、非对称遮挡和参考系不一致等问题。

Method: 作者构建 AirGroundBench，包含 11 个高保真仿真环境、1,021 对同步空地观测，生成约 62,000 个双视角四选一视觉问答实例和 115 个闭环视觉语言导航回合。任务覆盖 10 类，组织为四个能力维度：空间感知、跨视角对齐、空间变换与推理、具身决策；同时提供跨视角对象身份、2D/3D 度量边界框等结构化空间标注。

Result: 对 13 个代表性 MLLM 在 UAV-only、UGV-only 和 dual-view 输入设置下评测显示：模型在基础空间感知上表现较好，但在跨视角对齐和依赖空间变换的推理上明显受限，这些缺陷进一步影响序列式视觉语言导航决策。双视角输入相比单视角有可测提升，但仍显著落后于人类表现。

Conclusion: 当前 embodied MLLM 的关键短板在于跨异构视角的几何一致性建模不足。AirGroundBench 为诊断和推动多视角空间智能、空地协作导航以及几何约束推理提供了系统化评测平台。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied intelligence, yet their ability to maintain geometrically consistent spatial understanding across heterogeneous views remains under-evaluated. Existing benchmarks largely focus on single-agent, single-view perception, leaving a gap in the systematic assessment of collaborative air-ground settings, where multi-scale observations are complementary but introduce scale mismatch, asymmetric occlusion, and reference-frame inconsistencies. We present AirGroundBench, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating multi-view spatial intelligence in heterogeneous UAV-UGV collaboration. AirGroundBench is built from 11 high-fidelity simulated environments with 1,021 synchronized air-ground observation pairs, yielding approximately 62,000 dual-view, four-option single-choice visual question answering instances and 115 closed-loop vision-language navigation episodes. It covers 10 task types organized into four progressively demanding capability dimensions: spatial perception, cross-view alignment, spatial transformation and reasoning, and embodied decision-making. To support geometry-grounded evaluation and analysis, we provide structured spatial annotations, including cross-view object identities and metric 2D and 3D bounding boxes. Evaluations of 13 representative MLLMs under UAV-only, UGV-only, and dual-view input settings reveal consistent bottlenecks: models perform relatively well on spatial perception but struggle with cross-view alignment and transformation-intensive reasoning, and these deficits propagate to sequential decision-making in vision-language navigation. Although dual-view inputs provide measurable gains over single-view variants, a persistent gap from human performance remains, highlighting geometric consistency as a key limitation of current embodied MLLMs.

</details>


### [81] [ReScene: Structured Indoor Scene Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28060)
*Haoran Xu,Lechao Zhang,Daoguo Dong,Yan Gao,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReScene 是一个面向具身智能的多视角 3D 场景重建与组装框架，通过贯穿全流程的多视角几何先验，实现更准确、更物理一致且更高效的仿真就绪场景构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景构建方法存在依赖专用采集硬件、单视角物体重建偏差、布局几何合理但物理不一致等问题。作者指出核心瓶颈并非单物体重建，而是跨视角关系融合与物理合理的场景装配。

Method: 方法包含两个主要模块：1）HierView 根据语义一致性与 3D 覆盖完整性选择优先重建视角，替代传统最大掩码启发式；2）Relation-Aware Assembly 将视觉语言模型的多帧关系预测与几何、房间外壳先验融合为置信度加权场景图，用于物理一致的场景装配。

Result: 在 ScanNet 场景上，ReScene 在几何、渲染和感知质量方面达到新的最佳性能；相较最强基线，Chamfer Distance 降低 17%，LPIPS 降低 26%，并且相比既有多视角方法最高可快 10 倍。基于重建场景还生成了具身视觉问答数据集，微调后的 Qwen-VL 在若干空间推理任务上接近强闭源模型表现。

Conclusion: ReScene 表明，将多视角几何作为统一先验贯穿场景重建与关系装配流程，可以有效提升仿真就绪 3D 场景的几何精度、视觉质量、物理一致性和运行效率，并能进一步支持具身空间推理数据构建。

Abstract: Constructing simulation-ready 3D scenes from multi-view captures is a key bottleneck for Embodied Artificial Intelligence, as downstream tasks require object-level structure, explicit inter-object relations, and physical plausibility. Existing approaches either rely on specialized capture hardware, suffer from single-view bias in object reconstruction, or yield layouts that are geometrically reasonable but physically inconsistent. We identify that the problem is not single-object reconstruction but cross-view relation fusion and physically plausible scene assembly. To address this challenge, we present ReScene, a framework that threads multi-view geometry throughout the pipeline as a unifying prior. Our method consists of two main components: HierView prioritizes reconstruction views based on semantic consistency and 3D coverage completeness, replacing the largest-mask heuristic that conflates image occupancy with object coverage; and Relation-Aware Assembly fuses multi-frame relation predictions from a vision-language model with geometric and room-shell priors into a confidence-weighted scene graph, enabling physically consistent scene assembly. ReScene sets a new state of the art across geometry, rendering, and perceptual quality on a set of ScanNet scenes, achieving a 17% reduction in Chamfer Distance and 26% in LPIPS over the strongest prior baseline, while running up to 10x faster than prior multi-view methods. Based on the reconstructed scenes, we also generate an embodied visual question answering dataset, on which fine-tuned Qwen-VL approaches the performance of strong closed-source models on several spatial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [82] [TextDS: Parameter-Efficient Representation Alignment for Scene Text Detection under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28077)
*Boyuan Chen,Zichen Dang,Chuang Yang,Lap-pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextDS 是一个面向分布偏移的高效场景文本检测框架，通过双编码器、SWLoRA 与 CSF 在少量可训练参数下提升跨域与退化成像条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中的场景文本检测会遇到训练分布之外的域变化和成像退化；现有方法常依赖大规模场景文本预训练，且对跨域变化和真实退化条件的评估不足。

Method: 提出基于视觉基础模型的数据高效双编码器架构，避免大规模场景文本预训练；设计 Step-wise LoRA adaptation（SWLoRA）进行渐进式低秩特征细化，并结合动态早退机制；提出 Common Subspace Fusion（CSF）在共享子空间中对齐并融合双分支特征，同时保留互补的抗偏移信息；构建恶劣成像条件下的场景文本检测数据集用于评估。

Result: 实验表明，TextDS 仅使用 4.9M 可训练参数即可在场景文本检测中取得有竞争力的性能，并在跨域和不良成像条件下表现出较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作说明利用视觉基础模型、参数高效适配与共享子空间融合，可以在不依赖大规模场景文本预训练的情况下提升场景文本检测器对分布偏移的适应能力。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, scene text detectors inevitably face distribution shifts beyond the training distribution. Prior work often depends on large-scale scene-text pretraining, yet evaluation under cross-domain changes and real-world imaging degradations remains limited. We propose TextDS, an efficient framework for scene text detection under distribution shifts. First, we propose a data-efficient dual-encoder design with visual foundation models, eliminating the reliance on large-scale scene-text pretraining. Second, we introduce Step-wise LoRA adaptation (SWLoRA), which performs progressive low-rank refinement with a dynamic early-exit mechanism for effective feature adaptation. Third, we propose Common Subspace Fusion (CSF) to align and fuse the two branches in a shared subspace while retaining complementary, shift-robust information. Finally, we construct adverse-condition scene text detection datasets to address the gap in evaluating under imaging degradation. Experiments show that TextDS achieves competitive performance in scene text detection, demonstrating robustness across domains and adverse imaging conditions with only 4.9M trainable parameters.

</details>


### [83] [RPM-Distill: Physiology-guided Adaptive Cross-modal Distillation for Robust Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28089)
*Jiyao Wang,Qingyong Hu,Duoxun Tang,Xiao Yang,Kaishun Wu,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RPM-Distill：训练时利用同步 RF 雷达作为教师信号进行跨模态蒸馏，推理时仅需视频输入，以提升远程生理测量在光照、肤色和运动变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频式远程生理测量易受光照、肤色和运动影响；RF 雷达对外观和光照不敏感，可提供互补的心肺微运动信息，但推理阶段部署雷达成本高、普及度低。因此需要一种只在训练时利用雷达、推理时保持视频单模态的方法。

Method: 作者观察到 RGB 与 RF 信号虽物理机制和时域形态不同，但在频域中共享相似的潜在周期节律。基于此，提出生理结构化频谱蒸馏损失，包括基频峰锚定、非峰值背景分布匹配、频谱形态与尖锐度保持。同时设计 spectral policy network，根据学生与教师的频谱关系图预测样本级蒸馏门控和各损失权重，并通过小规模有标签验证集上的元双层优化学习，以缓解教师质量和模态对齐不确定性导致的负迁移。

Result: 在具有挑战性的条件和跨数据集实验中，RPM-Distill 相比视频单模态基线实现了 81% 的 MAE 改善和 21% 的相关性提升。

Conclusion: RPM-Distill 证明了训练阶段引入 RF 雷达频域生理先验可以有效增强视频 RPM 的鲁棒性，同时避免推理阶段对雷达硬件的依赖，是一种兼顾性能与部署可行性的跨模态蒸馏框架。

Abstract: Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) is highly accessible but remains fragile under varying illumination, skin tones, and motion. Radio frequency (RF) radar is largely invariant to illumination and appearance, providing complementary cardio-respiratory micro-motion cues; however, requiring radar at inference is often impractical due to its limited ubiquity and deployment overhead. We propose RPM-Distill, a physiology-guided cross-modal distillation framework that leverages synchronized radar only during training while retaining video-only inference. Our key observation is that although RGB and RF waveforms differ in sensing physics and time-domain morphology, they share similar latent periodic rhythm in the frequency domain. We thus distill physiology-structured spectral evidence to improve robustness, via losses that (i) anchor the fundamental peak, (ii) match the off-peak background distribution, and (iii) preserve spectral morphology and sharpness. To avoid negative transfer under sample-level teacher quality and alignment uncertainty, a spectral policy network predicts sample-level distillation gates and component weights from the student--teacher spectral relation map, learned with a meta bilevel objective on a small labeled validation split. Through extensive experiments in challenging conditions and cross-dataset settings, RPM-Distill brings 81\% MAE and 21\% correlation improvement over unimodal baselines. Code is at https://github.com/WJULYW/RPM-Distill.

</details>


### [84] [Diffusion Model Attribution via Spectral Coupling of Denoiser Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28092)
*Pragati Shuddhodhan Meshram,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spectral Denoising Signatures（SDS），通过刻画扩散模型去噪过程中的频谱能量重分布特征，实现对生成图像源模型的非侵入式归因，并在多模型与跨域提示场景下取得高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像的源扩散模型归因对来源验证和知识产权保护很重要，但不同数据集训练的扩散模型可能学习到相似的 score function 和输出分布，使得仅依据生成图像本身难以可靠归因；现有非侵入式方法在架构相近或共享自动编码器时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 作者提出 SDS，通过向候选扩散模型输入受控频率扰动，观察其标准前向去噪过程中对不同空间频段能量的重分布模式，从而提取模型固有的频谱去噪签名；方法不需要反演、优化或生成阶段注册。

Result: 实验显示 SDS 在八个多样化扩散模型上达到约 99.9% 归因准确率，在跨域 prompt shift 下达到 96.2%，并优于现有非侵入式基线，覆盖训练数据、架构和训练流程变化。

Conclusion: 扩散模型去噪 score function 的频谱几何具有稳定且可区分的模型指纹，可作为实用、原则性的非侵入式扩散模型归因依据。

Abstract: Attributing a generated image to its source diffusion model is a fundamental challenge in provenance verification and intellectual property protection. This problem is particularly difficult because diffusion models trained on different datasets can converge to similar score functions and thus similar output distributions, making the generated images themselves unreliable as attribution evidence. Existing non-invasive methods either fail on architecturally similar variants or rely on signals that vanish when models share the same autoencoder. We propose Spectral Denoising Signatures (SDS), a non-invasive attribution method that identifies the source model by fingerprinting each candidate model's denoising behavior. Our key insight is that a model's denoising score function exhibits a distinctive spectral geometry, reflected in how it redistributes energy across spatial frequency bands during denoising. By probing this behavior with frequency-controlled perturbations, SDS extracts a stable signature that is intrinsic to the model, requiring only standard forward passes with no inversion, optimization, or generation-time enrollment. Our results demonstrate that SDS achieves approximately 99.9% accuracy across eight diverse diffusion models and 96.2% under cross-domain prompt shift, outperforming non-invasive baselines across variations in training data, architecture, and training procedure, establishing spectral geometry as a principled and practical basis for diffusion model attribution. Code is available at: https://github.com/Pragati-Meshram/SGS

</details>


### [85] [Cross-view Multimodal Vision-Based Assessment Framework for Traditional Chinese Medicine Rehabilitation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28104)
*Francis Xiatian Zhang,Hao Yao,Shengxuan Chen,Hong Zhu,Hongxiao Jia,Sisi Zheng,Hubert P. H. Shum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CME-AQA，一个跨视角、多模态的视觉动作质量评估框架，用于中医康复训练技能评估；通过融合视觉与姿态信息并利用第一/第三人称双视角训练，在针刺、推拿等复杂手部操作任务上提升评估准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理治疗自动动作质量评估方法多依赖单视角骨架数据，难以处理针刺、推拿等中医技术中的手部自遮挡、复杂手-物交互和环境上下文缺失问题，因此需要更适合结构化临床技能训练的视觉评估方案。

Method: 作者提出 CME-AQA 框架，进行跨视角、多模态视觉评估：融合视频视觉特征与姿态信息以增强环境与动作理解；训练阶段利用同步第一人称和第三人称视频以提高推理鲁棒性；同时构建两个双视角数据集 TCM-AQA61-A（针刺）和 TCM-AQA61-T（推拿），包含 61 名受试者及专家标注。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个关键评分任务上达到优于或接近竞争基线的平均性能；在针刺深度、快速进针等关键指标上，相比最佳竞争方法的加权 F1 相对提升超过 10%；在插入时间、操作频率等定量指标上降低平均绝对误差；在 CPR 数据集上的测试也显示其在部分姿态评价标准上具有可比表现。

Conclusion: CME-AQA 能有效提升结构化中医康复训练中的动作质量评估准确性和稳定性，并可能推广到其他以人体动作为核心的模拟临床技能评估场景，为便捷、低成本、训练导向的技能评价提供支持。

Abstract: Vision-based assessment can provide convenient and cost-effective evaluation in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) rehabilitation training, where action quality assessment (AQA) from computer vision offers a promising solution. Existing automatic AQA frameworks for physical therapy typically rely on skeletal data captured from a single viewpoint, which is inefficient for TCM techniques such as acupuncture or Tuina that involve dense hand self-occlusion and complex hand-object interactions. To address these challenges, we propose CME-AQA, a cross-view, multimodal vision-based assessment framework that integrates visual-pose fusion to enhance understanding of environmental context and leverages both first-person and third-person videos during training to improve inference robustness. We collected two dual-view datasets, TCM-AQA61-A (Acupuncture) and TCM-AQA61-T (Tuina), each containing synchronized first-person and third-person recordings of 61 subjects with expert annotations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior or comparable mean performance against competitive baselines, achieving over 10% relative improvement in weighted F1 over the best competing method on key rating tasks such as Needle Depth and Quick Needle Insertion, while also reducing mean absolute error in quantitative measures such as insertion time and manipulation frequency. Testing on a CPR dataset further demonstrates comparable performance on several posture-based criteria, suggesting applicability to related structured simulated clinical skill assessments where participant motion is central to evaluation. Overall, CME-AQA enhances assessment accuracy for structured TCM rehabilitation training and facilitates more convenient and effective training-oriented skill evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [Monocular Avatar Reconstruction via Cascaded Diffusion Priors and UV-Space Differentiable Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28144)
*Hong Li,Minqi Meng,Yanjun Liang,Chongjie Ye,Houyuan Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Xianda Guo,Guojun Lei,Xuhui Liu,Chaojie Yang,Yanlun Peng,Hao Zhao,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据高效的单图像可重光照3D头像重建框架，利用预训练扩散模型与级联LoRA在UV空间完成纹理补全、去光照与PBR材质分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张自然图像重建高保真、可重光照的3D头像是高度病态问题，主要受限于高质量PBR数据稀缺，以及从输入纹理中分离光照与固有材质属性的难度。现有方法常依赖碎片化流程或大量专有数据，泛化与可复现性受限。

Method: 方法基于统一预训练扩散骨干网络，在UV空间为不同子任务训练级联LoRA：首先用Inpainting LoRA补全遮挡导致的缺失UV纹理；随后使用Light-Homogenization LoRA去除烘焙光照，并引入Cross-Intrinsic Attention协同生成像素对齐的PBR贴图，包括Albedo、Normal、Roughness、Specular与Displacement。分解阶段加入UV空间可微BRDF shading loss，以约束生成结果符合渲染方程并提升物理一致性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在少于100个真实3D扫描数据上训练，即可生成完整的4K分辨率PBR资产；相比现有先进方法，在真实感、泛化能力和材质完整性方面表现更优。作者还承诺论文接收后公开训练代码与模型权重。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了利用强预训练扩散先验与轻量LoRA适配，可以在低数据条件下有效解决单图像3D头像PBR重建中的纹理缺失、光照消除和材质分解问题，为高保真可重光照数字人资产生成提供了更可复现且数据高效的方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity, relightable 3D avatars from a single in-the-wild image is a challenging ill-posed problem, primarily hindered by the scarcity of high-quality PBR data and the complexity of disentangling illumination from intrinsic materials. In this paper, we present a data-efficient framework that leverages the robust priors of a unified pre-trained diffusion backbone to sequentially address texture completion, delighting, and material decomposition. Unlike existing methods that rely on fragmented pipelines or extensive proprietary datasets, we utilize cascaded Low-Rank Adaptations (LoRAs) to adapt the strong generative prior of the diffusion model for each sub-task in UV space. Specifically, we first employ an Inpainting LoRA to complete missing UV textures caused by occlusion, leveraging the model's semantic understanding to generate semantically and photometrically coherent details. Subsequently, a Light-Homogenization LoRA and a novel Cross-Intrinsic Attention mechanism are introduced to remove baked-in lighting and collaboratively synthesize pixel-aligned PBR maps (Albedo, Normal, Roughness, Specular, and Displacement). To ensure physical plausibility, we impose a UV-space differentiable BRDF shading loss during the decomposition stage, forcing the generative process to adhere to the rendering equation without the artifacts typical of rasterization-based supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, trained on fewer than 100 real 3D scans, generates comprehensive, 4K-resolution PBR assets with superior realism and generalization compared to state-of-the-art methods, and all training code and model weights will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [87] [EchoSonar-R: A Multi-View Reasoning-Enabled Model for Disease Classification and Report Generation in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28164)
*Darya Taratynova,Ahmed Aly,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EchoSonar-R：一个用于超声心动图的多视图推理型视觉语言模型，可同时进行多标签疾病分类与结构化报告生成，并提供基于解剖区域的可解释证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图诊断需要整合多个心脏切面中的互补信息。现有模型多关注分类性能，缺少显式诊断推理和空间定位的解剖证据，导致临床可信度与可解释性不足。

Method: 模型结合时空视频编码器与结构感知心脏检测器，引入空间定位的解剖线索以支持跨视图推理。训练分两阶段：先在带推理标注的目标上进行监督微调（SFT），再使用带任务特定奖励的 Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），在统一强化学习框架中联合对齐分类和报告生成任务。

Result: 在一个私有多视图数据集和两个公开基准上取得优于最强基线的表现：私有集宏平均平衡准确率提升 17.1%，MIMICEchoQA 提升 6.1%；报告生成达到 GREEN 临床忠实性分数 0.800，并能生成基于多视图视觉证据的可解释推理轨迹。

Conclusion: EchoSonar-R 通过多视图推理、结构化解剖定位和联合强化学习优化，提高了超声心动图疾病识别与报告生成的性能和可解释性，有助于增强临床使用中的信任度。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used non-invasive cardiac imaging modality, providing essential information for cardiovascular diagnosis. Interpreting an echocardiogram requires synthesizing complementary evidence across multiple heart views to identify abnormalities and produce structured clinical reports. While recent efforts focus on improving classification performance, most models lack explicit diagnostic reasoning and spatially grounded anatomical evidence, limiting clinician trust. We present EchoSonar-R, a multi-view reasoning-enabled vision-language model that jointly performs multi-label disease classification and report generation from echocardiography studies. EchoSonar-R combines a spatiotemporal video encoder with a structure-aware cardiac detector that provides spatially grounded anatomical cues to improve interpretability and clinician trust during cross-view reasoning. EchoSonar-R is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on reasoning-annotated targets, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific rewards that jointly align classification and report generation within a unified reinforcement-learning framework. Across a private multi-view dataset and two public benchmarks, EchoSonar-R improves macro balanced accuracy by 17.1% on the private set and 6.1% on MIMICEchoQA over the strongest baseline, achieves a GREEN clinical faithfulness score of 0.800, and produces interpretable reasoning traces grounded in multi-view visual evidence.

</details>


### [88] [RSICCLLM: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28266)
*Yelin Wang,Zijia Song,Shuo Ye,Chuanguang Yang,Miaoyu Wang,Yong Xu,Zhulin An,Yongjun Xu,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSICCLLM：面向遥感图像变化描述（RSICC）的首个大型视觉语言模型后训练框架，通过指令数据、差异感知监督微调和双负样本偏好优化提升细粒度变化理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSICC方法多依赖传统深度学习架构，模型容量有限；通用领域的大模型后训练方法难以直接迁移到RSICC，因为该任务存在数据稀缺、需要精细识别双时相遥感图像差异等挑战。

Method: 作者构建数据生成范式，发布RSICI指令数据集并建立任务专用基准；提出Difference-aware Supervised Fine-tuning以显式提取变化表征并引导模型理解时序差异；进一步提出Dual-Negative Preference Optimization（DNPO），通过两类互补负样本构建偏好数据集RSICP并优化模型输出。

Result: 大量实验表明，RSICCLLM在仅使用7B参数的情况下取得突出性能，并超过若干参数规模更大的模型。

Conclusion: 该工作证明针对RSICC设计的大模型后训练框架能够有效弥补数据和细粒度变化理解不足的问题，为遥感变化描述任务提供了可复现的数据、基准和方法体系。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to describe changes between bi-temporal remote sensing images and holds significant research and application value. However, most existing methods rely on conventional deep learning architectures, and the limited model capacity constrains performance. Although large-model post-training techniques have achieved great success in general domains, their direct transfer to RSICC remains challenging due to data scarcity and the need for fine-grained change understanding. To address this, we propose RSICCLLM, the first post-training framework for large vision-language models in RSICC. Specifically, we design a data generation paradigm, release the instruction dataset RSICI, and establish a task-specific RSICC benchmark. We further introduce Difference-aware Supervised Fine-tuning to explicitly extract change representations and guide the model in perceiving and understanding temporal differences. In addition, we propose Dual-Negative Preference Optimization (DNPO), which employs two complementary negative-sample construction strategies to construct the preference dataset RSICP and further refine model performance. Extensive experiments validate the superior capability of RSICCLLM, which achieves outstanding results with only 7B parameters, surpassing models of substantially larger scales. The code and dataset will be made publicly available at https://github.com/keaill/RSICCLLM.

</details>


### [89] [StructSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from Uncalibrated Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28321)
*Jia-Chen Zhao,Beiqi Chen,Xinyang Chen,Guangcong Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StructSplat 是一种前馈式、可泛化的 3D Gaussian 重建框架，可直接从未标定图像进行重建，无需相机参数，并在多个基准上显著优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D Gaussian 重建方法通常依赖逐场景优化或已知相机位姿；同时，许多方法在统一骨干网络中混合建模几何与外观，导致重建精度和跨场景泛化能力受限。因此，论文希望在无相机参数条件下实现高保真、可泛化的重建。

Method: 论文提出结构化表示，将几何、语义与纹理线索分配到明确的重建角色中。核心组件包括：像素对齐特征注入机制，用于从 2D 观测中精确建模纹理；语义感知先验，用于提升全局一致性；相机对齐策略，用于避免信息泄漏并增强泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明 StructSplat 在挑战性基准上显著优于先前方法。在 DL3DV 上达到 28.045 PSNR，相比 AnySplat 的 22.377 提升 5.67 dB；跨数据集评估中，在 ACID 上较 AnySplat 提升 1.94 dB，在 RealEstate10K 上提升 1.72 dB。

Conclusion: StructSplat 通过结构化地解耦和组织几何、语义、纹理信息，在无标定图像输入下实现了更高质量且更具泛化性的 3D Gaussian 重建，尤其适合未知相机参数和跨数据集场景。

Abstract: We present StructSplat, a feed-forward and generalizable 3D Gaussian reconstruction framework that operates directly on uncalibrated images without requiring camera parameters. Existing methods either rely on per-scene optimization or assume known camera poses, and often entangle geometry and appearance within a unified backbone, limiting reconstruction fidelity and generalization. Our key idea is to adopt a structured representation that organizes geometry, semantic, and texture cues with explicit roles in the reconstruction process. Specifically, we introduce a pixel-aligned feature injection mechanism to enable accurate texture modeling from 2D observations, incorporate semantic-aware priors to improve global consistency, and design a camera alignment strategy to prevent information leakage and improve generalization. Experiments show that our method significantly outperforms prior approaches on challenging benchmarks. On DL3DV, our method achieves 28.045 PSNR, surpassing AnySplat (22.377) by +5.67 dB. In cross-dataset evaluation, our method achieves +1.94 dB over AnySplat on ACID and +1.72 dB on RealEstate10K. Project page: https://structsplat.github.io Code: https://github.com/J-C-Zhao/StructSplat

</details>


### [90] [PerceptionRubrics: Calibrating Multimodal Evaluation to Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28322)
*Yana Wei,Hongbo Peng,Yanlin Lai,Liang Zhao,Kangheng Lin,En Yu,Keyu Lv,Han Zhou,Yin Tang,Haodong Li,Mitt Huang,Hangyu Guo,Jianjian Sun,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptionRubrics提出一种基于细粒度规则的感知评估框架，用门控评分暴露多模态模型在密集视觉场景中的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉/多模态评测基准分数趋于饱和，且多依赖整体语义匹配，难以反映模型在真实复杂场景中对关键事实和细节的可靠感知能力。

Method: 构建包含1,038张信息密集图像和12,000余条实例级rubric的数据集；通过Circular Peer-Review共识流程生成高质量黄金描述，并将其蒸馏为Must-Right必要事实与Easy-Wrong易错细节双流规则；采用Gated Scoring机制，对必要事实错误施加强二元惩罚。

Result: 实验发现模型虽能识别碎片化元素，但在严格组合约束下表现显著下降；开源与闭源前沿模型之间仍存在约8%的感知差距；门控指标相比传统评测更符合人类严格判断。

Conclusion: 严格的原子级视觉事实审计比整体语义匹配更能揭示模型真实感知可靠性，PerceptionRubrics为评估可靠生成与复杂场景理解提供了更具人类一致性的框架。

Abstract: We introduce PerceptionRubrics, a rubric-based evaluation framework that addresses the gap between saturated benchmark scores and real-world brittleness. Shifting evaluation from holistic semantic matching to rigorous atomic auditing, PerceptionRubrics pairs 1,038 information-dense images with over 12,000 instance-specific rubrics. These criteria are derived from golden captions constructed via a novel Circular Peer-Review consensus pipeline and then distilled into a dual-stream system of Must-Right (essential facts) and Easy-Wrong (fine-grained details) rubrics. Crucially, PerceptionRubrics implements a Gated Scoring mechanism: unlike linear averages, failure on mandatory visual facts triggers sharp binary penalties. Extensive evaluation yields critical insights: (1) The Reliability Gap: models often verify fragmented elements correctly yet fail strict conjunctive constraints, exposing brittleness in dense domains; (2) Open-Closed Stratification: contrary to reasoning trends, we reveal a persistent 8% perception deficit between open-source and proprietary frontiers; and (3) Human-Aligned Rigor: our gated metrics substantially out-align conventional benchmarks, validating that strict perceptual fidelity is the prerequisite for reliable generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Formalizing Latent Thoughts: Four Axioms of Thought Representation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27378)
*Fahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种用于评估大语言模型潜在思维表征的公理化框架，发现现有候选表征普遍无法同时满足四项功能性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估往往依赖下游任务准确率，容易将表征质量与模型处理能力混淆，导致难以判断失败究竟来自表征本身还是后续模型能力。

Method: 作者形式化提出四个功能公理：因果性、最小性、可分性和稳定性，并为每个公理设计可直接在表征上计算、独立于下游准确率的定量指标；随后在开放权重大语言模型和23个推理任务上进行审计。

Result: 实验发现没有任何候选表征能同时满足四项公理；这些表征可以可靠区分任务类型，但难以区分同一任务内的不同问题；同时其包含的信息很少超过输入嵌入中已有的信息。该现象在稠密模型、推理蒸馏模型和强化学习训练模型中均一致出现。

Conclusion: 潜在思维表征当前存在结构性缺陷，而非单纯由模型规模或训练过程造成；仅依赖下游基准准确率不足以揭示此类表征层面的失败。

Abstract: We introduce an axiomatic evaluation framework for latent thought representations in LLMs, comprising metrics that are independent of downstream benchmark scores and reveal representational failures that benchmark accuracy masks. Existing evaluations conflate representation quality with model capacity. Therefore, failures cannot be attributed to the representation rather than to the model that processes it. We formalize four functional axioms (Causality, Minimality, Separability, and Stability) and define a quantitative measure for each, computed directly on the representation independently of downstream accuracy. We audit open-weight LLMs across 23 reasoning tasks (e.g., Spatial Reasoning, Factual QA). We find that no candidate satisfies all four axioms simultaneously, that the representations distinguish task type reliably but cannot distinguish between two questions within the same task, and that the representations encode little information beyond what is already present in the input embedding. The failure is consistent across dense, reasoning-distilled, and RL-trained model families, indicating that the gap is structural rather than a property of model size or training procedure.

</details>


### [92] [Position: The Term "Machine Unlearning" Is Overused in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27379)
*Sangyeon Yoon,Yeachan Jun,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张在大语言模型研究中应严格限定“机器遗忘”的使用范围：仅用于由数据集明确定义的删除任务，即消除指定忘记集的训练影响，使模型近似等同于未使用该数据重训后的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着监管删除义务、版权/许可争议以及安全与产品策略需求增加，LLM 被要求“遗忘”某些数据、知识或行为。但现有研究将许多目标不同的任务都称为“unlearning”，导致概念混乱、保证不一致和评估错配。

Method: 该文为立场论文，主要通过概念澄清与论证区分不同任务类型：将真正的机器遗忘限定为数据集定义的删除，并指出拒答、有害请求抑制、实体/知识移除、目标行为压制等更适合归类为对齐、抑制、编辑或混淆等任务；同时强调应使用与目标相匹配的基线、指标和参考模型。

Result: 论文指出，术语混用会导致不同论文在同一标签下隐含不同保证；常用指标和基准被跨范围误用，可能奖励表层不披露现象，如低 ROUGE 或低遗忘准确率，却没有验证与重训模型的等价性，也无法证明派生能力已被消除。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁建立更严格的术语体系，将术语与显式保证和参考模型绑定；同时要求评估方法必须匹配所声称的目标，避免把策略性拒答、知识抑制或模型编辑误称为机器遗忘。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly face demands to "forget" training data, knowledge, or behaviors due to regulatory deletion obligations, copyright/licensing disputes, and safety or product-policy requirements. This position paper argues that machine unlearning is overused as a term in LLM research and should be reserved for dataset-defined deletion: removing the training influence of a precisely specified forget set such that the resulting model is approximately indistinguishable from retraining without that data. We contend that many tasks currently labeled "unlearning" (e.g., refusal for harmful requests, entity/knowledge removal, or targeted suppression) pursue different, often policy-dependent objectives and therefore require different terminology and baselines (e.g., alignment, suppression, editing, obfuscation). We further argue that this confusion is not cosmetic: because papers make different implicit guarantees under the same label, metrics and benchmarks are frequently reused outside their intended scope, rewarding surface-level non-disclosure (e.g., low ROUGE/forget accuracy) even when retraining-equivalence is not tested and derived capabilities remain. We conclude by calling for stricter terminology tied to explicit guarantees and reference models, and for evaluations that match the claimed objective.

</details>


### [93] [A Survey of Automated Presentation Coaching: Systems, Methods, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27380)
*Wen Liang,Li Siyan,Zackary Rackauckas,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述自动化口头报告辅导系统，提出五维任务分类法，并总结现有技术路线与关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化演讲辅导涉及计算机辅助发音训练、韵律建模和语音合成等方向，但此前缺乏从这些维度对现有系统进行系统梳理和比较的研究。

Method: 论文综述并分类了发音辅导、流利度与韵律训练、多模态训练、会议问答练习等系统；提出涵盖音段发音、词汇重音、超音段韵律、语速节奏和内容忠实度的五维任务分类法，并将已有系统映射到该框架；同时回顾TTS示范生成及发音、韵律、流利度诊断方法。

Result: 综述揭示了现有自动演讲辅导系统在任务覆盖上的差异与空白，并总结出常用技术主要集中在基于TTS的示范生成以及发音、韵律、流利度评估诊断。

Conclusion: 该领域仍面临标注演讲语料稀缺、跨不同母语背景的口音公平反馈、以及实时排练中低延迟诊断等核心挑战。

Abstract: Automated coaching for oral presentations sits at the intersection of computer-assisted pronunciation training (CAPT), prosody modeling, and speech synthesis, yet no prior work has systematically surveyed and compared existing systems along these dimensions. This survey reviews and categorizes automated presentation coaching systems, spanning pronunciation tutors, fluency and prosody coaches, multimodal trainers, and conference Q&A practice tools. We introduce a five-dimensional task taxonomy - covering segmental pronunciation, lexical stress, suprasegmental prosody, pacing, and content faithfulness - and explicitly map surveyed systems onto it to reveal coverage gaps. We further review the core technical methods these systems employ: TTS-based exemplar generation and diagnostic methods for pronunciation, prosody, and fluency assessment. Key open challenges include the scarcity of annotated presentation corpora, achieving accent-fair feedback across diverse L1 backgrounds, and delivering low-latency diagnostics for real-time rehearsal.

</details>


### [94] [Causal Connections: Leveraging Multilingual Fine-Tuning for Financial QA@FinCausal 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27446)
*Akash Kumar Gautam,Serhii Hamotskyi,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍 HSA_CORAL 团队在 FinCausal 2026 共享任务中的系统，用抽取式问答方法从英西双语金融叙事中抽取因果关系；最佳方案为在英西合并数据上微调的 GPT-4.1 Mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本中的因果关系识别对风险分析、事件解释和决策支持具有价值，但跨语言金融因果抽取面临语言差异、领域术语和标注数据有限等挑战，因此需要比较不同模型范式及适配策略。

Method: 论文比较三类方法：基于 multilingual BERT 的 encoder-only 序列标注、基于 multilingual BART 的 encoder-decoder 生成模型，以及 Llama 3.1 和 GPT 系列 decoder-only LLM；同时考察提示优化、少样本示例和监督微调，并使用英西合并训练数据进行多语言任务适配。

Result: 提示工程和少样本示例已能取得有竞争力的表现，但监督微调带来最大提升。最佳系统 GPT-4.1 Mini 在英西合并数据上微调后，在英文子任务中以 4.8140 并列最高，在西班牙语子任务中以 4.7753 排名第三，评测采用共享任务的 LLM-as-a-judge 指标。

Conclusion: 结果表明，针对任务的监督适配和多语言微调能显著提升金融因果问答性能，并有助于跨语言迁移；decoder-only LLM 在适当微调后优于传统编码器或生成式基线。

Abstract: This paper describes team HSA_CORAL's submission to the FinCausal 2026 shared task on extracting cause-effect relations from financial narratives via extractive question answering in English and Spanish. We compare three modeling families: (i) encoder-only token tagging with multilingual BERT, (ii) encoder-decoder generation with multilingual BART, and (iii) decoder-only LLMs (Llama 3.1 and GPT variants) using prompt refinement, few-shot demonstrations, and supervised fine-tuning. Across settings, prompting and few-shot examples yield competitive performance, while supervised fine-tuning provides the largest gains. Our best system, GPT-4.1 Mini fine-tuned on combined English and Spanish training data, achieves a tied highest score on the English subtask (score 4.8140) and ranks third on Spanish (score 4.7753) under the shared task's LLM-as-a-judge metric. Overall, the results highlight the value of task-specific adaptation and multilingual fine-tuning for cross-lingual transfer in financial causality QA.

</details>


### [95] [Developmental approach reveals the statistical learning of Neural Language Models: Transformers generalize from the most abstract statistical patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27460)
*Wang Bojun,Holly Jenkins,Elizabeth Wonnacott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过发展性视角研究生成式 Transformer 神经语言模型在合成语法学习过程中的统计学习轨迹与内部表征变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少揭示神经语言模型在训练不同阶段如何逐步形成语言统计知识与心理表征；作者希望借助可控合成语法和训练过程快照，分析模型语言认知能力的形成顺序。

Method: 训练一系列生成式 Transformer 模型学习合成语法，并在训练过程中多个阶段保存模型状态；随后分析不同阶段内部表征的变化，以追踪模型对全局抽象统计知识与局部统计依赖的习得过程。

Result: 模型在学习初期优先获得最抽象的全局统计知识，之后才逐渐习得相对局部的统计依赖；早期学习伴随大量过度泛化现象，而这些过度泛化会在后期训练中逐步受到约束。

Conclusion: 神经语言模型的统计学习并非简单地由局部到全局累积，而呈现先抽象全局、后细化局部的动态发展路径；基于此，作者提出了一个解释 NLM 统计学习与语言认知的新框架。

Abstract: In this study, we use a developmental approach to investigate the statistical learning and mental representation of neural language models (NLM). A series of Generative Transformer models are trained on a synthetic grammar. The model states are saved at multiple stages in the course of training. Through analyzing how the internal representations of these models change in the developmental path, we found that NLMs acquire the most abstract global statistical knowledge at the beginning of learning and later acquire the relatively local statistical dependencies. This learning path contains many over-generalizations from the very beginning and these over-generalizations are gradually constrained in the later stage of learning. Based on this observation, we propose a new framework to explain the statistical learning and language cognition of NLMs.

</details>


### [96] [Supersede: Diagnosing and Training the Memory-Update Gap in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27472)
*Vedant Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在长程、多会话对话中维护“当前事实”的能力，发现其主要瓶颈不是理解能力而是记忆维护；提出Supersede强化学习环境并初步证明该缺口可通过训练缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间交互中事实会被更新或替换，例如地址、价格、计划等发生变化。智能体需要使用最新事实并丢弃过时事实，但现有模型在有限自维护记忆条件下容易保留陈旧信息。论文旨在将这种“事实被替代后的当前性”能力独立测量，并探索其是否可训练。

Method: 作者在LongMemEval的knowledge-update子集上比较完整上下文与有限自维护记忆的表现，分析模型规模、对话长度和记忆容量对准确率的影响；随后发布Supersede强化学习环境，将回答当前值作为奖励、使用过时值作为惩罚；最后用GRPO对Qwen2.5-3B进行微调，并在未见真实对话上评估泛化表现。

Result: 在前沿模型gpt-5.4上，完整上下文准确率为92%，有限自维护记忆降至77%，差异显著（paired McNemar p<0.005）。随着对话长度增长24倍，准确率从68%降至28%；按比例增加记忆容量未带来可检测恢复（28%到28%，n=25）。GRPO微调Qwen2.5-3B后，保留测试集上的supersession准确率从9.0%提升到16.7%，且检查点曲线单调上升。

Conclusion: 论文表明，LLM智能体在动态事实环境中的核心失败是记忆维护与事实时效性判断，而非单纯上下文理解或记忆容量不足。Supersede提供了面向时间事实当前性的可训练环境，初步证明该能力缺口可以通过强化学习训练降低。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents operate over long, multi-session interactions in which facts change: a user moves, a price updates, a plan is revised. Acting correctly requires using the current value of a fact and discarding values that have been superseded. We isolate this ability on real conversational data and show that it is a distinct, unsolved failure. On the knowledge-update subset of LongMemEval, replacing an agent's full context with a bounded, self-maintained memory drops accuracy from 92% to 77% even on a frontier model (gpt-5.4), a gap that is statistically significant (paired McNemar p<0.005) and persists across model scale while full-context accuracy saturates near 92%. The bottleneck is therefore memory maintenance, not comprehension, and is not closed by a stronger model. We then ask whether this is merely an undersized memory, and find it is not: as the conversation grows 24x, accuracy falls further (from 68% to 28%), and granting the agent proportionally more memory yields no detectable recovery (28% to 28%, n=25). The failure scales with the length of the conversation, not the compression ratio. We release Supersede, an open reinforcement-learning environment (on the verifiers / prime-rl stack) that turns this measurement into a training signal: agents are rewarded for answering from the current value and penalized for stale ones. Finally, we close the loop and show the gap is trainable: GRPO fine-tuning a small open model (Qwen2.5-3B) on this environment nearly doubles its held-out supersession accuracy on real, unseen conversations (9.0% to 16.7%, a single run), along a monotonic checkpoint curve indicating the learned policy, not the harness, carries the gain. To our knowledge this is the first trainable environment whose reward targets temporal fact-currency, and the first evidence the supersession gap can be trained down, not only measured.

</details>


### [97] [The Context-Ready Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27538)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Context-Ready Transformer：通过在进入 Transformer block 前对 token 进行预上下文化，使浅层/单层模型在推理速度和困惑度上优于更深的标准 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 在自回归生成中每个 token 通常以原始嵌入进入层堆栈，依赖深层结构逐步整合上下文，导致推理计算成本高；作者希望构建一种能缓存历史上下文摘要并在当前 token 进入 block 前注入上下文的架构，以提升浅层模型能力和顺序推理效率。

Method: 设计由 D 层 Transformer block 构成的 Context-Ready Transformer。左到右生成时，correction network 将前一位置的 block 输出（过去上下文的缓存摘要）与当前 token embedding 结合，得到预上下文化表示后再输入 block；因此顺序推理形成 RNN 式 correction chain。训练时将 correction 过程在全序列上展开 K 次，每一步并行处理所有位置。还可通过添加零初始化 correction FFN 并微调，将预训练 Transformer 转换为 context-ready 模型。

Result: 在不同宽度、深度、block 大小和两个数据集上比较标准 Transformer、变体与消融。D=5 模型优于 12 层 Transformer，且在 A100 上生成快 1.7 倍；K=10 时，D=1 单层模型优于 6 层 Transformer，推理加速 2.6 倍；顺序推理与并行 K=10 的 PPL 差距小于 0.01。该架构在宽表示和长上下文中收益最大。在 pointer-chasing 任务中，D=1 结合 BPTT 可解决全部 10 个组合层级，而标准 Transformer 显示阶梯式深度依赖。

Conclusion: Context-Ready Transformer 通过显式的预上下文化和循环式上下文校正，将部分深层上下文整合能力转移到输入前处理阶段，使浅层模型也能有效利用长上下文，并在保持性能的同时显著提升自回归推理速度。

Abstract: We introduce the context-ready transformer, a new recurrent neural network architecture built from a D-layer transformer block that pre-contextualizes each token before it enters the block. During left-to-right generation, a correction network combines the previous position's block output -- a cached summary of past context -- with the current token embedding, so the tokenenters the block already contextualized rather than as a raw embedding. At sequential inference, the correction chain makes the architecture a recurrent neural network. For training, we unroll the correction process K times over the full sequence, processing all positions in parallel at each step. A pretrained transformer can also be converted to a context-ready model by adding a zero-initialized correction FFN and fine-tuning. We evaluate across widths, depths, block sizes, and two datasets, with all comparisons against standard transformers, variants, and ablations. A D=5 model beats a 12-layer transformer while generating 1.7x faster on an A100. With K=10, a single-layermodel (D=1) beats a 6-layer transformer with a 2.6x inference speedup, and sequential inference matches parallel K=10 to within 0.01 PPL. The architecture benefits most from wide representations and long contexts. On a pointer-chasing task, D=1 trained with BPTT solves all 10 composition levels, while standard transformers exhibit staircase-like depth dependence.

</details>


### [98] [EntMTP: Accelerating LLM Inference with Entropy Guided Multi Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27550)
*Carrie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EntMTP 是一种无需训练的熵引导多 token 预测调度方法，可根据局部生成熵动态选择推测解码的树形注意力拓扑，从而提升生成吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有使用 MTP 头的模型通常在整个生成过程中采用固定的树形注意力拓扑，导致推测深度和验证计算量恒定；这与自然语言中低熵片段适合更深推测、高熵片段需要保守推测的特性不匹配。

Method: 论文提出 Entropy-guided Multi-Token Prediction（EntMTP），一种 training-free 调度器。它维护局部生成熵的运行估计，并在一组任务特定的 Pareto 最优树形注意力拓扑中动态切换，使推测深度与上下文可预测性相匹配。

Result: 在 Humaneval、ShareGPT、GSM8k 和 Litbench 基准上，EntMTP 相比 Hydra 持续获得约 1.15× 加速，相比 Medusa 的峰值加速达到 1.36×，同时不牺牲生成质量。

Conclusion: EntMTP 表明基于生成熵的动态推测调度能够更好适配文本生成中的可预测性变化，在无需额外训练的情况下提升多 token 预测和自推测解码效率。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction has been shown to increase data density during training, improve downstream text-generation quality, and serves as the defacto approach for self-speculative decoding. Existing foundation and open source models that use MTP heads commit to a static tree-based attention topology throughout the entire generation sequence, meaning the speculation depth, and thus the compute required during verification, stays constant regardless of the context. This is fundamentally misaligned with the entropy patterns of natural language where low-entropy regions often support reliable multi-step drafting, while high-entropy regions require more conservative speculation. To address this, we propose Entropy-guided Multi-Token Prediction (EntMTP), a training-free scheduler that toggles between tree-based attention topologies from a set of task-specific pareto-optimal trees conditioned on a running estimate of local generation entropy. By matching speculation depth to context predictability, EntMTP maximizes expected accepted-token throughput across the full distribution of generated text without sacrificing generation quality. When evaluated across Humaneval, ShareGPT, GSM8k, and Litbench benchmarks, EntMTP consistently achieves a 1.15x speedup against Hydra and peak speedup of 1.36x against Medusa baselines respectively.

</details>


### [99] [Ko-WideSearch: A Korean Breadth-Search Benchmark for Exhaustive Set Enumeration by Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27595)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ko-WideSearch，一个面向韩语网页代理的广度搜索基准，用于评估完整枚举实体集合及填写属性表的能力。实验显示，现有网页代理通常能找回集合成员，但难以准确完成整行属性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页代理基准主要评估“深度”搜索，即通过多步约束找到单个隐蔽答案；而“广度”搜索——完整枚举封闭集合并填写每个条目的属性——评估不足，尤其缺少英语之外的场景。同时，广度基准的黄金答案构建成本高，需要验证集合完整性与表格单元正确性。

Method: 作者构建了 Ko-WideSearch：一个韩语广度搜索基准，采用自动化 synthesize-and-verify 流程生成与验证任务。每个任务给定一个集合父实体，要求输出完整成员及逐项属性表。基准包含 190 个实体、228 张表、16 个类别和三个难度层级；难度由表宽和二维复合键两个结构因素控制。评估指标包括 Item-F1、Column-F1 和 Row-F1，并使用归一化感知比较器统一用于黄金答案构建与评分。

Result: 在 20 个网页代理上的实验显示，代理在成员召回上表现较好，但整行属性准确性明显不足，例如 Item-F1 为 92.8，而 Row-F1 仅为 53.7。随着表宽和复合键难度增加，性能持续下降；增加搜索次数或成本也不能有效弥合差距。按单元分析表明，主要困难在于找到正确值，而不是格式化；开放式自由文本单元最容易失败，而日期、姓名等标准答案单元通常表现较好。

Conclusion: Ko-WideSearch 揭示了当前网页代理在广度搜索和结构化信息填表方面的系统性短板。未来提升网页代理能力不仅需要更强的搜索覆盖，还需要更可靠的逐项属性抽取、验证和表格级一致性建模。

Abstract: Web-agent benchmarks overwhelmingly measure depth -- pinning one obscure answer behind a chain of constraints -- while breadth, exhaustively enumerating a closed set and filling each item's attributes, is barely evaluated, especially outside English. Breadth is also hard to build: certifying that a gold set is complete and every cell correct is far costlier than checking a single answer. I introduce \textsc{Ko-WideSearch}, a Korean breadth-search benchmark built by an automated synthesize-and-verify pipeline. Each task names a set-parent entity -- a TV season, a dynasty, a league, an administrative region, an election -- and asks for its full membership plus a per-item attribute table, graded by Item-, Column-, and Row-F1. It spans 228 tables over 190 entities and sixteen categories across three difficulty tiers, set by two structural knobs I dial independently -- table width and a 2-D composite key -- so cross-product membership climbs from 0\% to 100\% across the tiers. A single normalization-aware comparator is shared between gold construction and grading, so stable date and count columns are not over-dropped on formatting alone. Across twenty web agents, the failure is consistent: agents recover the set but not the rows (e.g.\ Item-F1 92.8 against Row-F1 53.7), accuracy falls steadily as the knobs harden, and neither more search nor more spend closes the gap. Broken down by cell, the hard part is finding the right value, not formatting it: open-ended free-text cells fail most, while cells with a standard answer such as a date or a name usually come out right.

</details>


### [100] [Narrative-UFET: Narrative Generation for Ultra-Fine Entity Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27598)
*Mreedul Gupta,Advait Deshmukh,Ashwin Umadi,Matt Pauk,Maria Leonor Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Narrative-UFET，通过为实体提及自动生成短篇连贯叙事来扩展超细粒度实体类型标注，证明叙事级上下文比句子级上下文更有助于识别长尾类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超细粒度实体类型标注方法主要依赖句子级上下文，难以处理长尾类型；而实体类型判别所需证据往往分散在多句文本中。由于现有UFET资源多为句子级，难以系统检验篇章/叙事上下文的作用。

Method: 作者构建 Narrative-UFET：为每个实体提及配对自动生成的短篇连贯叙事，并设计两个受控变体：Maintain 中实体类型在叙事中保持不变，Change 中实体类型随叙事发生变化。通过与句子级基线和自然上下文进行对比，评估受控叙事上下文对长尾类型预测的影响。

Result: 叙事上下文相较句子级基线在长尾类型上带来稳定提升；其中 Change 变体提供更强信号。与自然出现的上下文相比，合成叙事带来的增益更明显。

Conclusion: 受控构造的叙事上下文能显化真实文本中隐含的判别信号，有助于长尾超细粒度实体类型识别；但仍有较大提升空间，未来可继续改进篇章建模与叙事生成方法。

Abstract: Ultra-fine entity typing (UFET) assigns highly specific types to entity mentions, but current approaches struggle with types in the long tail. We hypothesize that a key limitation is the reliance on sentence-level context, since disambiguating evidence is often spread across multiple sentences. Testing this has been difficult because all existing UFET resources are sentence-level. We present Narrative-UFET, a controlled extension of UFET in which each entity mention is paired with an automatically generated short, coherent narrative. Synthesizing narratives lets us isolate the effect of specific discourse properties. We experiment with two paired variants: one in which the entity's type is held constant across the narrative (Maintain) and one in which it shifts (Change). We show that narrative context yields consistent improvements on long-tail types over sentence-level baselines, with the Change variant providing the stronger signal. A comparison against naturally occurring contexts shows that synthetic narratives yield stronger gains, indicating that controlled discourse construction can surface signals that real text leaves implicit. Substantial room for improvement remains, suggesting open directions in both discourse modeling and narrative construction.

</details>


### [101] [Masked Language Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27617)
*Iskander Azangulov,Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Leo Zhang,Simon Vary,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Masked Language Flow Models（MLFMs），将掩码机制融入 Flow Language Models，以兼顾并行生成效率与多步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MDMs 虽支持快速并行生成，但其反向转移在 token 位置上独立分解，在少步采样时近似误差较大；FLMs 通过连续流实现甚至单步生成，但必须一次性解码所有 token，难以处理需要逐步推理的复杂任务。

Method: 作者引入 MLFMs，使用连续随机插值在部分掩码序列与干净序列之间建立桥接，将掩码条件生成纳入连续流框架；同时提出轻量适配方法将预训练 MDMs 转换为 MLFMs，并设计一种交替执行连续去噪与离散高置信 token 解掩码的新采样器。

Result: 在 GSM8K 和 MT-Bench 上的实验显示，该方法使基于 flow 的语言模型首次能够扩展到下游推理和指令跟随任务。

Conclusion: MLFMs 结合了 MDMs 的掩码式逐步生成优势与 FLMs 的连续流建模能力，为高效并行生成和复杂推理任务之间提供了新的折中方案。

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) promise fast, parallel language generation, but their reverse transition factorises across token positions -- an approximation that breaks down in the few-step sampling regime where parallel generation ought to provide the greatest efficiency gains. Flow Language Models (FLMs) sidestep this limitation by learning a continuous flow that transports noise toward clean sequences represented in Euclidean space, inducing a flow map that can be distilled for single-step generation. However, this makes complex tasks requiring multi-step reasoning problematic for FLMs, as FLMs are forced to decode every token during generation. To address this, we introduce Masked Language Flow Models (MLFMs), which incorporate masking into FLMs using a continuous stochastic interpolant to bridge partially masked and clean sequences. This design enables conditional generation via continuous flows and allows pretrained MDMs to be converted into MLFMs through a simple, lightweight adaptation. Leveraging this flexibility, we propose a novel sampler that alternates continuous denoising with the discrete unmasking of confident tokens to better support multi-step reasoning. We evaluate our approach on GSM8K and MT-Bench and find, for the first time, that flow-based language models can be scaled to solve downstream reasoning and instruction-following tasks.

</details>


### [102] [Cross-Platform Chinese Offensive Comment Detection via Dual-Threshold Hard Example Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27629)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Fangfang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中文社交媒体跨平台冒犯性评论检测的性能退化问题，提出双阈值难例挖掘方法，以低成本标注实现跨平台领域适应并提升多平台检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文冒犯性评论检测模型在从源平台迁移到微博、小红书、贴吧、知乎等不同平台时会因领域分布差异产生明显性能下降，因此需要系统刻画跨平台退化原因，并设计低成本、有效的领域适应策略。

Method: 研究首先使用 clean-Chinese-base RoBERTa 在 COLD 数据集上微调，建立二分类基线；随后构建覆盖四个平台的三分类细粒度测试集，并用 Jaccard 距离与 Proxy-A Distance 量化源域与目标域差异；在此基础上提出双阈值难例挖掘策略，基于预测置信度从未标注语料中筛选高置信与低置信的易错样本，再结合少量人工标注难例进行二次微调。

Result: 实验表明，经过双阈值难例挖掘与二次微调后的模型在微博、小红书、贴吧、知乎四个平台上均取得显著性能提升，相比基线缓解了跨平台领域迁移带来的性能退化。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过置信度驱动的难例选择与少量人工标注，可以以较低成本实现中文冒犯性评论检测的跨平台领域适应，为社交媒体文本分类任务中的领域迁移问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cross-platform deployment of offensive comment detection for Chinese social media suffers performance degradation. The paper proposes a dual-threshold hard mining method to address this. First, the clean-Chinese-base RoBERTa is finetuned on COLD to establish a binary baseline for fair comparison. Second, a three-class fine-labeled test set covering Weibo, Xiaohongshu, Tieba, and Zhihu is constructed, domain distances from the source are quantified using Jaccard and Proxy-A Distance, as well as the degradation bottleneck of the baseline under domain shift is systematically revealed. Herein, a dual threshold hard example mining strategy is proposed. High- and low-confidence error-prone samples are filtered from unlabeled corpora by prediction confidence. The model is secondarily finetuned under implicit contexts with merely a small set of manually labeled hard examples, realizing low-cost cross-platform domain adaptation. Experiments reveal significant performance gains of the optimized model across four platforms.

</details>


### [103] [Yuvion LLM: An Adversarially-Aware Large Language Model for Content And AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27632)
*Ting Ma,Xiufeng Huang,Benlei Cui,Xiaowen Xu,Shikai Qiu,Ruijie Jian,Hongxing Li,Guanghui Wang,Longtao Huang,Haiwen Hong,Haolei Xu,Wenjing Jiang,Ziwen Xu,Zhaoyu Fan,Shaoxuan He,Chuxi Xiao,Yujian Li,Xinyue Chen,Chunyang Chai,Wenxuan Liu,Ziheng Wang,Dongjie Zhang,Yangfan Zhou,Libin Dong,Yupeng Cao,Xiaoqian Xia,Jing Wang,Zhe Jiang,Zhenan Ye,Guang Yang,Bin Liu,Wei Peng,Ziqiang Zhu,Meihui Lian,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Haidong Ding,Bingyu Zhu,Yan Wang,Hai Zhao,Xuan Jin,Wei Zhao,Pengfei Sun,Wei Wang,Huiming Zhang,Bin Li,Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Yuvion LLM，面向对抗鲁棒内容安全与更广泛 AI 安全的语言模型，并配套提出包含 93 个基准的 YLRE 评测体系；结果显示其在安全与对抗场景中优于多个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到真实系统后仍可能出现安全失效。作者认为安全问题本质上具有对抗性，许多风险来自有策略地规避模型策略与防护机制；现有通用模型开发往往低估了真实部署中的规划、工具使用和多步推理安全挑战，导致评测结果高估实际鲁棒性。

Method: 构建 Yuvion LLM，将对抗鲁棒性与智能体能力作为核心目标。训练流程包括对抗感知的数据构建、知识增强的继续预训练、基于策略的多任务安全后训练、风险感知监督微调、基于强化学习的策略优化，以及面向工具使用和多步推理复杂安全场景的安全感知智能体强化学习。同时提出 YLRE，覆盖四类评估、共 93 个基准。

Result: 在 YLRE 及相关开放/内部评测中，Yuvion LLM 在安全导向任务上表现突出，尤其在对抗条件下展现较强鲁棒性，同时保持较好的通用能力。摘要称 Yuvion-8B 在若干安全任务上超过多数先进基线，甚至超过一些更大规模模型。

Conclusion: 该工作主张安全模型开发应显式面向对抗鲁棒性和智能体场景，Yuvion LLM 与 YLRE 展示了面向真实部署安全风险的训练与评测框架，并表明较小规模模型也可能通过安全专门化训练获得强安全表现。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in real-world systems, safety failures can still lead to harmful outputs and dangerous misuse. We argue that the essence of safety is adversarial: many failures arise not from natural inputs alone, but from strategic attempts to evade model policies and safeguards. However, existing general-purpose model development largely overlook this adversarial nature, and often remain insufficient for realistic safety scenarios involving planning, tool use, and multi-step reasoning, causing measured safety performance to overestimate real deployment robustness. To address this gap, we present Yuvion LLM, a large language model built for adversarially robust content safety and broader AI safety. Yuvion LLM treats adversarial robustness and agentic capability as first-class objectives. Its pipeline combines adversarially aware data construction, knowledge-enhanced continued pretraining, and policy-grounded multi-task safety post-training, including risk-aware supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization, together with safety-aware agentic reinforcement learning for tool use and multi-step reasoning in complex safety scenarios. We further introduce the Yuvion LLM RiskEval (YLRE), a collection of 93 benchmarks across four evaluation categories, covering diverse open and internal evaluations with a focus on safety, adversarial robustness, and real-world capability requirements. Across these evaluations, Yuvion LLM demonstrates clear advantages on safety-focused benchmarks and particularly strong robustness under adversarial conditions, while maintaining solid overall capability. Notably, Yuvion-8B outperforms most state-of-the-art baselines, including substantially larger models such as GPT-5.4 and Qwen3-MAX, on several safety tasks.

</details>


### [104] [When Search Agents Should Ask: DiscoBench for Clarification-Aware Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27669)
*Yiling Tao,Shihan Deng,Meiling Tao,Pengzhi Wei,Zhichao Hu,Zhihao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoBench 是一个面向澄清感知深度搜索的新基准，用于评估 LLM 搜索智能体在模糊、欠明确或含错误查询下主动识别歧义、提问澄清并恢复正确推理路径的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体基准通常假设用户查询完整且明确，忽略真实场景中请求常常模糊、信息不足或事实有误；在多步深度搜索中，这类歧义会沿推理链传播，导致错误检索轨迹。

Method: 作者构建 DiscoBench，包含 211 个样本、463 个歧义实例，覆盖 11 个真实领域和 4 类歧义；同时设计多轮交互用户模拟器，并从任务效用、歧义检测、交互策略和成本效率四个维度评估代表性 LLM。

Result: 实验表明，歧义检测与有效澄清是不同能力；许多模型即使能检索，也不一定能通过交互解决问题。反复搜索而不主动澄清，往往比直接猜测表现更差。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 搜索智能体在交互式问题解决方面仍存在关键短板，仅提升检索能力不足以应对真实深度搜索中的模糊查询；未来需要强化歧义识别、澄清提问与多轮纠错能力。

Abstract: Search agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex information-seeking tasks, requiring multi-step retrieval and reasoning to fulfill user goals. However, existing benchmarks often assume that user queries are complete and explicit, overlooking the fact that real-world search requests are frequently vague, underspecified, or even factually incorrect. In deep search scenarios, such ambiguity can propagate along multi-step reasoning chains and lead agents toward incorrect search trajectories. To address this gap, we introduce DiscoBench, a benchmark for clarification-aware deep search, designed to evaluate whether search agents can proactively identify ambiguity, ask effective clarification questions, and recover correct reasoning paths through user interaction. DiscoBench contains 211 samples and 463 ambiguity instances across 11 real-world domains, covering four ambiguity types. We further design a user simulator for multi-turn interaction and evaluate model performance from four perspectives: task utility, ambiguity detection, interaction strategy, and cost efficiency. Experiments on representative LLMs show that ambiguity detection and effective clarification are distinct capabilities, and that repeatedly searching instead of asking for clarification often performs worse than direct guessing, highlighting a critical gap between retrieval ability and interactive problem-solving in current search agents.

</details>


### [105] [From Signals to Transfer: A Factorised Study of Probe-Based Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27679)
*Ponhvoan Srey,Xiaobao Wu,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Quang Minh Nguyen,Duc Anh Vu,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于探针的LLM不确定性估计进行因子化、受控比较，发现域内原始隐藏状态/注意力特征强，但分布迁移下结构化和压缩特征更稳健，并提出可迁移的预训练探针基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于探针的不确定性估计方法在特征设计、训练数据构造和评估设置上同时变化，导致难以判断性能提升究竟来自哪些因素；同时，仅看域内表现不足以评估面向部署的可靠性。

Method: 作者在匹配条件下开展因子化实验，分别控制并比较不同内部特征表示、提示方式、标签构造和评估场景；在总结最佳实践后，基于基准数据训练预训练探针，并测试其向开放式事实生成任务的迁移能力。

Result: 实验显示，域内场景中原始隐藏状态和注意力特征很难被超越；但在分布偏移条件下，结构化、压缩后的特征具有更好的鲁棒性。提示设计和标签构造会显著影响探针行为。预训练探针能够较好迁移到开放式事实生成。

Conclusion: 论文认为探针式不确定性估计的进展应通过更受控、更面向部署的评估来衡量；域内性能不是充分指标，鲁棒性、迁移能力以及稳定的离线基线同样关键。

Abstract: Probe-based uncertainty estimation (UE) has emerged as a prominent approach to detect hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) by learning uncertainty from internal model signals. Yet, recent methods vary simultaneously across feature design, training data construction, and evaluation setting, obscuring what actually drives performance. To address this issue, we propose a factorised study of probe-based UE under matched conditions. Our results show that raw hidden states and attention features are difficult to outperform in-domain. However, under distribution shift, structured and compressed features are more robust, suggesting that in-domain performance alone is insufficient to measure progress. Furthermore, prompting and label construction significantly affect probe behaviour. Building on these best-practice findings, we train benchmark-based pretrained probes that transfer reasonably well to open-ended factual generation, providing a stable off-the-shelf baseline. Our work encourages more deployment-oriented evaluation of probe-based uncertainty estimators. The code repository is available at https://github.com/ponhvoan/ProbeUE.

</details>


### [106] [Mitigating LLM-based p-Hacking by Preregistering for the Next LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27687)
*Maria Thomas,Kristina Gligoric,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于降低LLM研究中 p-hacking 风险的预注册协议：在当前模型上确定流程，预注册未来合格模型与分析计划，并在预注册后发布的首个合格模型上做确认性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常被用于生成、分类和标注数据，并进一步进入假设检验；但研究者可能通过反复调整提示词、解码参数或输出格式来获得期望显著结果，导致LLM研究容易发生 p-hacking。

Method: 作者设计协议：先在现有模型上完成实验流程与分析方案，预注册实验、分析计划及未来合格模型集合；随后等待预注册后发布的第一个合格LLM，并仅在该模型上运行确认性分析。作者还在两个已知真值任务、20个来自四家提供方的模型和11种LLM分析配置上进行评估，并开展压力测试与自我预注册实验。

Result: 在两个任务中，该协议分别阻止了73.9%和72.7%的 p-hack 成功迁移；压力测试下仍保持显著缓解效果。作者自己的预注册实验也显示，在先前模型上可被hack的7种配置中，有6种未能迁移到后续首个合格模型。

Conclusion: 该协议利用“未来模型在承诺时尚不存在”这一机制，降低研究者针对确认性模型调参造假的可能性；同时由于针对一个模型的hack往往不能稳定迁移到下一模型，因此该方法可作为LLM辅助研究中提升统计可信度和可复现性的实用防护措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate, classify, and annotate data whose outputs feed downstream hypothesis tests. However, LLM-based research is easy to p-hack: a researcher can tune the prompts, decoding parameters, or output format until a desired result is reached.
