We propose a protocol to mitigate p-hacking in LLM-based research: preregistering the experiment and eligible models, and then running it on the first eligible LLM that is released after the preregistration. The researcher finalizes the procedure on current models, preregisters the analysis plan together with a set of eligible future models, and runs the confirmatory analysis on the first eligible model released afterward. Because this model does not exist at commitment time, it cannot be hacked against; furthermore, configurations that hack one model frequently do not transfer to the next. We evaluate the protocol on two tasks whose true values are known. Across 20 models from four providers and 11 LLM-analysis configurations, the protocol would have blocked successful transfer of the p-hack in 73.9% and 72.7% of cases in the two tasks. Additional analyses reveal that mitigation remains substantial under several stress tests. Finally, putting money where our mouth is, we followed our own protocol and preregistered our experiment. The preregistered experiment confirmed the protocol's effectiveness: out of the 7 configurations that hacked the prior model, the hacking failed to carry over in 6 configurations on the first eligible model released afterward.

</details>


### [107] [Mitigating Position Bias in Transformers via Layer-Specific Positional Embedding Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27705)
*Changze Lv,Zhenghua Wang,Yiran Ding,Yixin Wu,Tianlong Li,Zhibo Xu,Muling Wu,Tianyuan Shi,Shizheng Li,Qi Qian,Xuanjing Huang,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LPES：为 Transformer 不同层分配不同 RoPE 位置缩放因子，以缓解长上下文中的“中间信息丢失”问题；无需微调、几乎不增加推理延迟，并在多项长上下文任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在长上下文输入中常对位于中间位置的关键信息关注不足，即 lost-in-the-middle。已有多尺度 RoPE 方法要么推理延迟高，要么依赖人工设计的缩放策略，难以获得稳定且高效的改进。

Method: 作者提出层特异性位置嵌入缩放方法 LPES，为每一层设置不同的位置缩放因子，以调整各层注意力分布。为高效搜索这些缩放因子，设计了遗传算法，并利用 Bézier 曲线参数化缩放因子序列，从而显著压缩搜索空间。该方法不更新模型参数，也不增加明显推理开销。

Result: 实验显示 LPES 能缓解位置注意力偏置，在多个长上下文基准上带来稳定提升；在 key-value retrieval 数据集上最高获得 11.2% 的准确率提升。

Conclusion: LPES 是一种轻量、免微调、低延迟的 RoPE 缩放优化方法，通过按层定制位置缩放策略改善长上下文信息利用，尤其有助于缓解中间位置关键信息被忽略的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with the ``lost-in-the-middle'' problem, where critical information located in the middle of long-context inputs is often underrepresented or lost. While existing methods attempt to address this by combining multi-scale rotary position embeddings (RoPE), they typically suffer from high latency or rely on suboptimal hand-crafted scaling strategies. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a layer-specific positional embedding scaling~(LPES) method that assigns distinct scaling factors to each layer. LPES achieves a more balanced attention distribution without fine-tuning model parameters or increasing inference delay. A specially designed genetic algorithm is employed to efficiently select the optimal scaling factors for each layer by incorporating Bézier curves to significantly reduce the search space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LPES effectively mitigates positional attention bias and delivers consistent improvements across multiple long-context benchmarks, yielding up to an $11.2$\% accuracy gain on the key-value retrieval dataset.

</details>


### [108] [Low-Agreeableness Persona Conditioning for Safe LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27709)
*Austin MY Cheung,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究“温暖/共情”微调导致的对抗安全性下降问题，并提出通过数据构造来兼顾对话温度与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现，将大语言模型微调得更具社会温暖感会降低事实可靠性并增加迎合性；本文进一步关注一个相关但不同的风险：温暖微调是否会削弱模型抵抗越狱和有害输出的能力。

Method: 作者提出一种 persona-driven rewriting pipeline：将用户轮次改写为低宜人性/更具对抗性的表达，同时配对温暖、降温式的助手回复；并在四个模型上进行三组实验，与通用温暖微调基线比较，同时使用表征探测分析潜在空间中“温暖方向”和“服从方向”的几何关系。

Result: 相较于通用温暖微调基线，该方法降低了模型对越狱攻击的易感性和有害输出率，同时保持了对话温暖性；表征探测显示，该数据条件化可能减少了潜在空间中温暖与顺从/服从方向的对齐。

Conclusion: 安全的共情式微调可以仅通过更精细的数据设计实现，而不必依赖额外安全标签、有害内容检测器或训练目标改动。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) for social warmth degrades factual reliability and increases sycophancy. We investigate a related but distinct failure mode: warmth fine-tuning also weakens adversarial safety, making models more susceptible to jailbreaks and harmful output generation. We examine whether this reflects an inherent consequence of empathetic adaptation or an artifact of data construction. To address this, we introduce a persona-driven rewriting pipeline that conditions user turns on low agreeableness and pairs this with warm, de-escalating assistant responses. Across three experiments on four models, our approach reduces jailbreak susceptibility and harmful output rates relative to generic warmth fine-tuning baselines, while preserving conversational warmth. Representational probing provides suggestive evidence that this conditioning reduces the geometric alignment between warmth and compliance directions in latent space. These results show that safer empathetic fine-tuning is achievable through data design alone, without safety labels, harm detectors, or changes to the training objective.

</details>


### [109] [Do Speech Emphasis Models Generalize across Languages and Emotions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27717)
*Megan Wei,Deepali Aneja,Jiaqi Su,Yunyun Wang,Haonan Chen,Zeyu Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMEE 多语言多情感重音语料库，并系统评测重音检测模型在语言、情感、数据集与数据规模变化下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重音检测研究主要依赖单语、中性、朗读语音，难以反映真实场景中跨语言、情感和说话风格的韵律重音变化，因此需要更具多样性的语料和全面评测。

Method: 构建包含 7 种语言、34 类情感/风格、10,000 条专业录制表达性语音、14.13 小时数据的 MMEE 语料库，并为每条样本提供 10 个感知标注形成三级重音标签；在单语、跨语、多语、跨情感、跨数据集和数据规模设置下评测两种先进模型架构。

Result: 单语模型零样本迁移能力有限，尤其在类型差异较大的语言间性能下降明显；多语训练显著提升稳健性；模型在高唤醒与低唤醒情感之间迁移较稳定；合成基准与感知基准之间存在双向迁移能力；较小训练规模下性能仍较稳健。

Conclusion: MMEE 为研究真实表达性语音中的重音检测提供了重要资源，结果表明多语与多情感训练可提升模型泛化能力，且不同情感和数据集之间共享一定韵律结构。

Abstract: Prosodic emphasis varies across languages, emotions, and speaking styles, yet existing emphasis detection models are largely trained and evaluated on monolingual neutral read speech. We introduce MMEE (Multilingual Multi-Emotion Emphasis), a corpus of 10,000 professionally recorded expressive utterances (14.13 hours) across 7 languages and 34 emotion/style categories, with three-level perceptual labels (10 annotations per sample). We benchmark two state-of-the-art architectures under monolingual, cross-lingual, multilingual, cross-emotion, cross-dataset, and data-scale settings. Monolingual models show limited zero-shot transfer, degrading across typologically distant languages, while multilingual training substantially improves robustness. Models transfer robustly between high- and low-arousal emotions; bidirectional transfer between synthetic and perceptual benchmarks suggests shared prosodic structure; and performance stays robust even at smaller training scales.

</details>


### [110] [Enhancing Numerical Prediction in LLMs via Smooth MMD Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27731)
*Zhuo Zuo,Li Yue,Wenhao Zheng,Chenpeng Wang,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Smooth Maximum Mean Discrepancy（SMMD）损失，用于提升大语言模型/视觉语言模型在数值精确输出任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵将数字 token 视为无结构类别，忽略数值之间的距离关系，导致模型在需要精确数值输出时表现不稳定。

Method: SMMD 在数值子词表上引入基于数值距离的核，并结合图平滑约束：一方面通过核匹配对齐预测数值分布与目标分布，另一方面在核诱导图上平滑预测-目标残差以增强局部一致性。

Result: 在数学推理、算术计算、时钟时间识别和图表问答四类数值目标任务上，跨多个开源 LLM/VLM 骨干模型，SMMD 相比交叉熵和近期数值目标损失均稳定提升准确率。

Conclusion: 显式建模数值 token 的度量结构对提升数值精确性很重要；MMD 项与平滑项具有互补作用，距离核设计是方法有效性的关键。

Abstract: Despite their strong general capabilities, large language models (LLMs) often remain unreliable when outputs must be numerically precise. A key reason is the training objective: standard cross-entropy treats numeric tokens as unstructured categories and ignores the metric structure of their values. We address this mismatch with Smooth Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SMMD), which builds on the classic MMD by incorporating value-distance kernels over numeric tokens and graph-based smoothness. With this kernel defined over a numeric sub-vocabulary, SMMD aligns the predicted numeric distribution to the target via kernel matching and smooths the prediction-target residual over the induced kernel graph to encourage local consistency. We evaluate SMMD on four numeric-target tasks: mathematical reasoning, arithmetic calculation, clock-time recognition, and chart question answering, across multiple open-weight LLM and VLM backbones. SMMD consistently improves accuracy over both cross-entropy and recent numeric-target losses; analyses show complementary effects between MMD and smoothness and underscore the importance of distance-based kernel design. Code is available at https://github.com/Zuozhuo/smmd-loss.

</details>


### [111] [KG2Cypher: Data-Centric Pipeline for Building Enterprise Text-to-Cypher Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27742)
*Minjun Choi,Yerin Kim,Junghyuk Seo,Sujin Mo,Hyemin Lee,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KG2Cypher 是一个面向企业知识图谱的 Text-to-Cypher 数据构建与训练流水线，通过从已有图谱事实生成可执行查询，再用 LLM 反向生成自然语言问题，显著提升企业私有图谱问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业知识图谱被广泛用于内部搜索、分析和问答，但为私有企业图谱构建自然语言接口成本高，尤其在韩语企业场景中，短查询、搜索式表达以及模式改写会造成语言 grounding 困难。

Method: 该方法先基于已观测图谱事实构造可执行 Cypher 查询，再利用 LLM 生成对应自然语言问题；随后通过 LLM judge 与人工验证筛选 Text-Cypher 对，并转换为 candidate-aware SFT 数据。推理阶段结合类别条件化 schema prompting、实体检索和 LoRA 推理。

Result: LoRA SFT 将广播节目查询的 execution-result F1 从 0.806 提升到 0.950，将公司查询从 0.70 提升到 0.92。在 11 类任务设置中，达到 95.2% exact match、99.9% execution rate 和 0.964 execution-result F1。

Conclusion: KG2Cypher 证明了以数据为中心、从图谱事实出发生成训练数据的策略，可以有效降低企业 Text-to-Cypher 系统构建成本，并在复杂企业韩语场景中获得高准确率和高可执行性。

Abstract: Enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are increasingly used for internal search, analytics, and question answering, but building natural-language interfaces for private enterprise graphs remains costly. We present KG2Cypher, a data-centric pipeline for building enterprise text-to-Cypher systems from existing KGs. KG2Cypher first constructs an executable Cypher query from observed graph facts and then uses LLMs to generate its associated natural-language question. The resulting Text-Cypher pairs are validated with an LLM judge and human validation, and are converted into candidate-aware SFT data. The trained generator is served with class-conditioned schema prompting, entity retrieval, and LoRA-based inference. We evaluate KG2Cypher in Korean enterprise settings, where short search-style queries and schema paraphrases make language grounding difficult. LoRA SFT improves execution-result F1 from 0.806 to 0.950 on broadcast-program queries and from 0.70 to 0.92 on company queries. In an 11-class setting, KG2Cypher achieves 95.2% exact match, 99.9% execution rate, and 0.964 execution-result F1.

</details>


### [112] [Output-Space Allocation Costs for Calibration-Guided LLM Compression: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27785)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在ROCKET类免训练大语言模型压缩中，将MCKP分配代价从权重空间误差改为与层输出重构目标一致的输出空间误差，是否能提升压缩后模型保真度。实验显示在高压缩率下零样本准确率小幅提升但困惑度变差，低压缩率下影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROCKET方法的层内分解目标基于输出重构，但跨层/跨选项资源分配时使用权重空间Frobenius误差作为代价，目标不一致。作者希望验证将分配代价与输出空间目标对齐是否能带来更好的压缩效果。

Method: 提出ROCKET-ActCost：保留ROCKET的稀疏字典分解与多选择背包问题分配框架，但将MCKP allocation cost替换为基于校准数据激活/输出空间的重构误差。分别在Qwen3-8B 50%压缩和Llama-3.2-1B 20%压缩下比较原ROCKET与ActCost，评估零样本基准平均准确率和WikiText困惑度。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B 50%压缩下，ROCKET-ActCost平均零样本准确率从52.3%提升到53.1%，提升0.8个百分点；但WikiText困惑度从52.98升至61.46，恶化约16%。权重空间误差与输出空间误差相关性超过0.99，导致分配方案差异有限。在Llama-3.2-1B 20%压缩下，两者结果接近：准确率53.3% vs 53.5%，PPL 14.45 vs 14.66。

Conclusion: 分配代价与输出重构目标对齐在高压缩率下可带来有限的下游准确率收益，但可能损害语言建模困惑度，说明不同分配目标偏向不同指标。由于两类误差高度相关，代价函数改变带来的实际分配差异和整体效果有限，尤其在较低压缩率下影响较小。

Abstract: Training-free compression methods for large language models (LLMs) often use calibration data to guide compression decisions. ROCKET, a recent method combining sparse-dictionary factorization with multi-choice knapsack problem (MCKP) allocation, derives its per-layer factorization from an output reconstruction objective but uses weight-space Frobenius error as the MCKP allocation cost. We investigate whether aligning the allocation cost with the output-space objective improves compressed model fidelity. On Qwen3-8B at 50\% compression, our ROCKET-ActCost achieves +0.8 percentage points higher average accuracy across 8 zero-shot benchmarks (53.1\% vs 52.3\%), but increases WikiText perplexity by 16\% (61.46 vs 52.98). This accuracy-perplexity tradeoff reveals that different allocation objectives favor different downstream metrics. The high correlation ($>$0.99) between weight-space and output-space errors limits allocation divergence, explaining the modest effect size. On Llama-3.2-1B at 20\% compression, the two methods produce near-identical results (53.3\% vs 53.5\% accuracy, 14.45 vs 14.66 PPL), suggesting that the effect of the cost function is minor at lower compression ratios.

</details>


### [113] [SHIFT: Gate-Modulated Activation Steering for Knowledge Conflict Mitigation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27786)
*Ruochang Li,Pengcheng Huang,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Huiyuan Xie,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 SHIFT，将 RAG 中知识冲突缓解从直接修改内部神经元转化为可学习门控调制，在冻结主干模型的前提下以极少参数自适应调节内部激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 系统中检索上下文与大模型参数化知识发生冲突时，会影响生成可靠性。现有神经元级知识编辑方法虽然试图增强模型依赖上下文证据的能力，但被修改神经元往往与更广泛模型能力纠缠，可能产生级联副作用并损害通用能力。

Method: 提出 SHIFT 框架，为 LLM 增加轻量级 gate module，并冻结主干模型，仅优化少于 0.01% 的可训练参数。生成过程中，门控模块调节模型内部表示，使模型能够在上下文知识与参数化知识之间进行自适应利用。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，SHIFT 相比多种竞争基线具有更好的知识冲突缓解效果。论文同时开源了数据集与代码。

Conclusion: SHIFT 提供了一种参数高效且较少侵入主干模型的 RAG 知识冲突解决方案，有望降低神经元编辑带来的副作用，同时提升模型对检索上下文的利用能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances LLMs by incorporating external knowledge to support response generation. However, conflicts between retrieved context and parametric knowledge have emerged as a critical challenge in RAG systems. To mitigate such conflicts, numerous studies have attempted to identify and edit knowledge-related internal neurons, aiming to improve the ability of LLMs to rely on contextual evidence during generation. However, these neuron-level approaches may introduce unintended cascading effects that compromise the general capabilities of LLMs, as the modified neurons are often entangled with broader model behaviors and functionalities. In this paper, we introduce SHIFT, a novel framework that reformulates neuron-level modification as learnable gate modulation, allowing LLMs to adaptively regulate internal activations for knowledge conflict resolution. Technically, our SHIFT equips LLMs with a lightweight gate module and optimizes fewer than 0.01% trainable parameters while keeping the backbone model frozen. During generation, the gate module adjusts the model's internal representations to adaptively leverage contextual and parametric knowledge. Extensive experiments on six datasets validate the effectiveness of our SHIFT in comparison with various competing baselines. All datasets and code are available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/SHIFT.

</details>


### [114] [NLL-Guided Full-Attention Layer Selection for Training-Free Sliding-Window Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27791)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的 NLL 引导层选择方法，用于在混合注意力长上下文推理中决定哪些层保留全注意力；在仅使用 1/4 全注意力层时接近 1/2 周期基线性能，并显著优于其他 1/4 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合全注意力与滑动窗口注意力可以降低长上下文推理成本，但关键问题是应在哪些层保留全注意力。现有固定周期或基于注意力的启发式方法不一定与下游任务准确率直接相关。

Method: 对每一层进行训练-free 校准：将该层从全注意力替换为滑动窗口注意力，并计算答案 token 的负对数似然（NLL）退化程度；NLL 退化越大表示该层对长程依赖和最终准确率越重要，应优先保留全注意力。

Result: 在 LongMemEval 与 Qwen3-4B 上，仅使用 1/4 全注意力层达到 64.6% 准确率，接近 1/2 全注意力周期基线的 65.0%；相比 SWAA 报告的周期 1/4 全注意力基线提升 10.4 个百分点，相比匹配的 LightTransfer-style 基线提升 26.4 个百分点。校准约需 15 分钟。

Conclusion: NLL 引导层选择能更直接地捕捉对下游准确率重要的层，并将选择信号与长程注意力需求关联起来，从而在长上下文 LLM 部署中改善效率—准确率 Pareto 前沿。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models that mix full and sliding-window attention across layers offer a promising approach to efficient long-context inference, but the critical question of \emph{which layers} should retain full attention remains unsolved. Existing methods use either fixed periodic patterns or attention-based heuristics that may not capture what matters for downstream accuracy. We propose NLL-guided layer selection, a training-free method that directly measures each layer's importance by computing the negative log-likelihood degradation on answer tokens when that layer uses sliding-window instead of full attention. On LongMemEval with Qwen3-4B, our method achieves 64.6\% accuracy using only 1/4 full-attention layers, matching the 1/2-FA periodic baseline (65.0\%) while halving the computational budget. NLL-guided selection outperforms the SWAA-reported periodic 1/4-FA baseline by 10.4 percentage points and a matched LightTransfer-style baseline by 26.4 percentage points. De-confounding analysis shows the signal is consistent with long-range attention needs rather than generic layer sensitivity. The method requires only $\sim$15 minutes of one-time calibration, advancing the efficiency-accuracy Pareto frontier for long-context LLM deployment.

</details>


### [115] [Position Bias Correction is Insufficient for One-Pass Attention Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27793)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting，试图通过估计并校正长上下文模型的提示级位置偏置曲线，实现单次排序以替代昂贵的迭代式 Attention Sorting；实验表明该方法未能达到迭代排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型存在位置偏置，导致中间位置的信息利用不足。已有 Attention Sorting 通过多轮“排序—生成”缓解该问题，但部署成本较高。作者希望验证位置偏置是否是主要瓶颈，并通过一次性去偏排序降低成本。

Method: 从低注意力的大多数文档中估计每个提示的 position-bias curve，并用该曲线对原始注意力分数进行校正，包括减法校正或除法校正；随后基于校正后的注意力分数进行单次文档排序，并在两个长上下文模型上评估。

Result: 在 LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct 上，去偏方法与未校准的单次排序结果相同，containment accuracy 为 94.83%；在 YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k 上，去偏提升 8.67 个百分点，但仍落后迭代排序 14.84 个百分点，仅弥合约 37% 的差距。

Conclusion: 实验否定了“位置偏置是主要瓶颈”的假设。单纯校正位置偏置不足以复现迭代式 Attention Sorting 的收益，重复重排序可能带来超出偏置校正之外的额外优化作用。

Abstract: Long-context language models suffer from position bias, where information in middle positions is underutilized. Attention Sorting addresses this by iteratively reordering documents based on attention patterns, but its multiple sort-and-generate cycles increase deployment cost. We hypothesize that position bias is the primary bottleneck and propose Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting, which estimates a per-prompt position-bias curve from the low-attention majority of documents and uses it to correct raw attention scores (via subtraction or division) to enable single-pass sorting. Our experiments on two models refute this hypothesis in the tested setting: on LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct, debiasing produces identical results to uncalibrated single-pass sorting (94.83\% containment accuracy), while on YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k, debiasing improves accuracy by 8.67 percentage points but remains 14.84pp behind iterative sorting, closing only 37\% of the gap. These results suggest that position-bias correction is insufficient to match iterative sorting, and that repeated reordering provides additional benefits beyond bias correction.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Complementary Action Modeling from Automotive Maintenance Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27808)
*Jiaqi Wu,Bai Li,Jochen Hartmann,Martin Gaedke,Sander Stuijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出互补动作建模（CAM）任务：在保持维护指令其余语境基本不变的情况下，通过修改动作短语识别或生成程序性互补指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车维护指令中，极小的词汇变化，尤其是动作短语变化，可能反转或改变指令的程序含义；传统句子相似度或同义改写方法难以捕捉这种细粒度程序关系。

Method: 基于德语汽车维护数据集，研究候选匹配与受控Seq2Seq生成；重点考察互补关系与表面相似性的区分、动作短语级别的生成控制，以及通过检索、重叠指标和人工评估衡量关系正确性。

Result: 实验表明，互补维护指令更适合被建模为由细微词汇线索支撑的程序性关联，而不是普通句子相似或同义改写问题。

Conclusion: CAM强调动作短语在程序语义中的决定性作用，为维护指令理解与生成提供了更精细的建模视角，并提示评估应关注关系正确性而非仅关注文本相似度。

Abstract: A minute lexical variation can reverse the procedural meaning of an instruction even when the rest of the sentence remains unchanged. In automotive maintenance instructions, this pattern often appears when an action phrase turns an instruction into its procedural counterpart. The entities, modifiers, and surrounding context remain largely invariant, while the action phrase determines the procedural relation. We define this task as Complementary Action Modeling (CAM). Given a maintenance instruction, the goal is to identify or generate its procedural counterpart by modifying the action phrase while preserving the remaining sentence context. This task focuses on three aspects: distinguishing complementarity from surface similarity, controlling generation at the action-phrase level, and evaluating relational correctness using retrieval, overlap-based, and human evaluation. Using a German automotive maintenance dataset, we examine these questions through candidate matching and controlled Seq2Seq generation. The results show that complementary maintenance instructions are best modeled as procedural associations grounded in subtle lexical cues. They should therefore not be treated as ordinary cases of sentence similarity or synonym-based paraphrasing.

</details>


### [117] [A Study of Temporal Fusion Strategies for Named Entity Recognition in Historical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27881)
*Emanuela Boros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将时间元数据结构化融入历史文本命名实体识别模型，发现晚期融合策略在跨时间泛化和噪声时期表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文本中的命名实体会随时间发生表面形式和重要性变化，给NER带来独特挑战；现有语言模型对时间性，尤其是历时语境中的推理能力仍有限，因此需要探索显式利用时间元数据的方法。

Method: 作者比较绝对与相对时间表示，并将其注入基于Transformer的NER架构中；采用多种轻量级融合策略，包括早期融合与晚期融合，以及交叉注意力、适配器和拼接等机制。

Result: 在法语和德语历史数据集上的实验表明，晚期融合策略整体更鲁棒，并具有更好的时间泛化能力，尤其在较早、噪声更大的历史时期表现突出。

Conclusion: 结构化引入时间元数据有助于提升历史NER模型的历时适应性；相比早期融合，晚期融合更适合处理历史文本中的时间漂移与数据噪声。

Abstract: Temporal variation poses a unique challenge for named entity recognition (NER) in historical texts, where entities drift in surface form and salience across time. While language models (LMs) have made progress in various NLP tasks, their ability to reason about temporality, especially in diachronic contexts, remains limited or at least, questionable. In this paper, we systematically study how temporal metadata can be structurally embedded into NER models using a range of lightweight fusion strategies. We experiment with both absolute and relative temporal representations, injected into Transformer-based architectures via early or late fusion mechanisms such as cross-attention, adapters, and concatenation. Our evaluations on French and German historical datasets reveal that late fusion strategies yield more robust and temporally generalisable performance, particularly in early and noisy periods.

</details>


### [118] [Triadic Werewolf: A Jester Role for Multi-Hop Theory of Mind in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27909)
*Avni Mittal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在狼人杀中加入“小丑”第三阵营，用三方效用冲突评估大语言模型的心智理论与多智能体推理能力；结果显示该设置能暴露传统二元社交推理游戏难以发现的推理缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型心智理论评测多依赖二元社交推理游戏，观测线索通常指向单一隐藏阵营，模型可能凭语言先验取得高分，而无需真正模拟对手激励与策略。

Method: 作者扩展狼人杀，引入以“被投票出局”为获胜目标的小丑阵营，形成村民、狼人、小丑三种相互冲突的效用函数；在GPT-4.1、DeepSeek-V3.1、Llama-3.3-70B上进行60局实验，并比较小丑自学习机制开启与关闭的表现。

Result: 小丑胜率达到60–70%，狼人胜率从未超过20%；GPT-4.1扮演狼人时在首日有60–70%的概率投出小丑，属于自我损害行为。自学习提升DeepSeek与Llama表现，却削弱GPT-4.1，且损失主要由村民而非狼人承担。只有DeepSeek学会“显得可疑但不显得故意可疑”的微妙策略，并从自学习中获益最多。

Conclusion: 三方激励结构比传统二元推理游戏更能揭示大语言模型在多智能体策略推理、对手效用建模和反直觉目标识别上的不足。

Abstract: Theory-of-mind evaluations of large language models typically use dyadic social-deduction games, where every observable cue points to a single hidden side, so a model with strong language priors can score well without ever simulating opponents' incentives. We extend the Werewolf game with a Jester, a third faction whose utility on peer suspicion is inverted because it wins by being voted out, so optimal play requires reasoning across three opposing utility functions. Across 60 games on GPT-4.1, DeepSeek-V3.1, and Llama-3.3-70B with Jester self-learning on and off, the Jester wins 60-70% of games while Werewolves never exceed 20%, and GPT-4.1 wolves vote the Jester out on day 1 in 60-70% of games, a strictly self-defeating action. Self-learning helps DeepSeek and Llama but hurts GPT-4.1, with the cost landing on Villagers rather than Werewolves. Only DeepSeek learns the subtle strategy of looking suspicious without looking intentionally suspicious, and it gains the most from the loop. Triadic incentive structure exposes a layer of multi-agent reasoning that dyadic deduction games leave invisible.

</details>


### [119] [VASAE: Naming SAE Dictionary Directions with Vocabulary-Aligned Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27941)
*Kairui Zhang,Ziwen Yu,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VASAE通过词汇对齐锚定训练稀疏自编码器，使特征在训练中获得内在token名称，并在多层Transformer残差流中保持与标准SAE相当的重构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE能分解Transformer残差流，但特征通常依赖事后人工或自动命名，缺乏与模型token词表的直接、内生联系；论文希望让SAE特征在学习过程中就与词汇空间对齐，从而提升可解释性。

Method: 提出Vocabulary-Aligned Sparse Autoencoder（VASAE），在SAE训练中加入词汇对齐锚定机制，并将每个特征命名为与该特征最近的token embedding对应的token字符串；通过最近token对齐分数评估特征与词表的对齐程度，并在GPT-2-small与Llama-3.1-8B残差流上实验。

Result: VASAE在不降低相较标准SAE重构质量的前提下产生词汇对齐字典。GPT-2-small第0到10层在0.8对齐阈值下约90%特征对齐；Llama-3.1-8B浅层代表字典有92.8%强对齐，中层也有强对齐，而最终层代表字典对齐有限。减去句子级平均稀疏编码后，案例显示许多内在token名称与邻近输入token相关。

Conclusion: 词汇对齐锚定可在训练阶段将SAE特征连接到内在token名称，作为传统事后解释方法的补充；该方法尤其适用于浅层和中层残差流特征解释，但最终层对齐效果受限。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide useful decompositions of Transformer residual streams, but their learned features are usually named post hoc rather than directly connected to the Transformer's token vocabulary. We introduce Vocabulary-Aligned Sparse Autoencoder (VASAE), a method that trains SAE features under vocabulary-aligned anchoring and assigns each feature an intrinsic token name: the token string whose embedding is nearest to that feature. Without reducing reconstruction quality compared with a standard SAE, VASAE produces dictionaries with vocabulary-aligned features. Using a 0.8 cutoff on the nearest-token alignment score, dictionaries trained on GPT-2-small post-residual streams align about 90% of features in layers 0--10. In Llama-3.1-8B, representative shallow and middle-layer dictionaries contain strongly aligned features, including 92.8% in the shallow layer, while the representative final-layer dictionary shows limited alignment. After subtracting the sentence-level mean sparse code, case studies show that many remaining intrinsic token names are relevant to nearby input tokens. These results suggest that vocabulary-aligned anchoring can connect learned features to intrinsic token names during training, complementing post hoc interpretation of learned dictionaries.

</details>


### [120] [An Empirical Analysis of Factual Errors in Human-Written Text and its Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27959)
*Kazuma Iwamoto,Kazumasa Omura,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测，提出基于报纸更正记录的人为事实错误分类体系，并评估大语言模型在该任务上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实错误检测研究因大语言模型兴起而更多转向LLM生成文本的幻觉检测，导致人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测相对被忽视。作者希望弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者分析报纸文章的更正记录，归纳人类诱发事实错误的分类体系；随后基于该分类构造合成的真实感测试样例，并结合真实更正数据，评估未经过专门微调的LLM在事实错误定位任务中的表现。

Result: 实验显示，即使是高性能LLM，在合成评测数据上的词级F1也仅达到52%，说明该任务具有较高难度。进一步按检测难度进行分析，揭示了当前LLM在不同类型事实错误检测上的能力边界。

Conclusion: 人类撰写文本中的事实错误检测仍是一个重要且困难的问题，其错误类型与LLM幻觉基准关注点不同；现有LLM尚不能可靠完成该任务，需要面向人为事实错误的专门数据、分类体系和检测方法。

Abstract: Factual Error Detection (FED), which is the task of identifying factually incorrect spans in a given text, has long been recognized as an important research problem. However, with the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), research attention has shifted toward factual errors specific to LLM-generated text (hallucinations) and their detection. As a result, the detection of factual errors in human-written text has been relatively neglected. To address this gap, we first distill a taxonomy of human-induced factual errors by analyzing corrections of newspaper articles, a representative source of text that is guaranteed to be human-written and contains few grammatical errors. Our analysis revealed that there are characteristic categories such as kanji misconversions and numeral classifier errors, which are not focused in existing hallucination benchmarks. Based on the taxonomy, we then evaluate the FED capability of vanilla LLMs on synthesized realistic test cases and real corrections. Experimental results demonstrated that even high-performance LLMs such as GPT-5.4 achieved only word-level F1 score of 52% on the synthetic evaluation data, highlighting the task difficulty. Furthermore, a detailed analysis by detection difficulty revealed the current state of FED.

</details>


### [121] [From Black-Box to Clinical Insight: A Multi-Stage Explainable Framework for Speech-Based Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27973)
*Yasaman Haghbin,Sina Rashidi,Ali Zolnour,Fatemeh Taherinezhad,Ali Fartoot,Hossein Azadmaleki,James M Noble,Maryam Dadkhah,Maryam Zolnoori*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向语音认知障碍检测的多阶段可解释性框架，将Transformer黑箱预测转化为临床可理解叙事，并在医生评估中显示较好一致性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音认知障碍检测具有非侵入、易获取、成本较低等优势，但现有Transformer模型虽然性能较好，却缺乏临床可解释性，限制其在真实医疗流程中的采纳。

Method: 框架整合SHAP token归因、理论驱动的语言学特征，以及基于LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct的四阶段推理流程；依托SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network多模态筛查模型，将模型输出映射到词汇丰富度、句法复杂度、语义连贯性等四类认知-语言维度。

Result: 基础筛查模型在NIA PREPARE基准上达到F1=72.11%；在70个分层英文样本上的医生评估显示，该解释框架与患者级认知画像高度一致；系统可用性量表得分为82/100。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将模型归因、语言学理论特征和大语言模型推理结合，可提升语音认知障碍检测模型的临床可解释性，并具有较好的临床工作流整合潜力。

Abstract: Speech-based cognitive impairment detection offers a noninvasive, accessible alternative to costly biomarker assays, yet transformer-based models remain clinically uninterpretable. We propose a multi-stage explainability framework that translates black-box transformer predictions into clinically grounded narratives by integrating SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based token attribution, theory-informed linguistic features, and a four-stage LLM reasoning pipeline using LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct. Built on the SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network multimodal screening model (F1 = 72.11% on the NIA PREPARE benchmark), the framework maps model outputs to four cognitive-linguistic dimensions, including lexical richness, syntactic complexity, and semantic coherence. Physician evaluation on 70 stratified English samples demonstrated strong alignment with patient-level cognitive profiles, and a System Usability Scale score of 82/100 indicated high potential for clinical workflow integration.

</details>


### [122] [ToxiREX: A Dataset on Toxic REasoning in ConteXt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27981)
*Stefan F. Schouten,Ilia Markov,Piek Vossen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We introduce a new, contextual, multilingual dataset called ToxiREX: Toxic REasoning in ConteXt. The dataset consists of threads of Reddit comments and structured characterizations of what the comments imply, following a systematic toxic reasoning schema developed in a previous paper. Using the schema allows us to capture and explain implicit and context-dependent toxicity, while supporting mappings to existing toxicity taxonomies. The dataset includes comments in six languages (English, Arabic, Turkish, Spanish, German, and Dutch), collected from posts connected to specific major events (e.g. the 2023 Turkey earthquakes; the Russian invasion of Ukraine). We describe the context-preserving preprocessing of the threads. We create a training set of 125 thousand comments which is annotated by a commercially available LLM, and a test set of just under three thousand comments that is annotated by native speakers. We show that apparent disagreements in the test set annotations often reflect defensible alternative interpretations rather than noise. Finally, we provide baseline results by prompting and fine-tuning language models. To produce these results, we develop evaluation strategies for our hierarchical, schema-based predictions. While models perform better than random, there remains a lot of room for improvement, showing the task to be challenging. ToxiREX is the first dataset to simultaneously incorporate multiple languages, conversational context, and implicit toxicity, while using the toxic reasoning schema for rich, structured annotations. Dataset available at: https://github.com/cltl/toxirex

</details>


### [123] [Dialogue to Detection: A Multimodal Hybrid NLP Pipeline for Insurance Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28002)
*Muhammad Shakeel Akram,Amal Htait,Abdul Hamid Sadka,Emma Meisingseth,Karishma Jaitly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于保险首次报案（FNOL）欺诈早期检测的合成多模态框架，生成对话文本与双说话人音频，并结合文本、检索、规则特征和说话人嵌入进行风险评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险欺诈造成显著经济损失与运营低效，而FNOL阶段的早期识别仍困难；现有研究多依赖私有、纯文本数据，限制了可复现性以及对语言、行为和说话人线索融合的多模态方法发展。

Method: 构建合成多模态FNOL环境，生成代理人与客户的对话转录文本及双说话人音频；执行自动语音识别与说话人分离；下游结合命名实体识别、正则特征抽取、LLM-RAG检索和说话人嵌入，并用规则化风险评分检测叙事复用、结构不一致和跨案件声音重复。

Result: 数据集验证与组件级评估显示该框架具有稳定性和一定迁移潜力，可在控制误报与敏感性的同时提供可复现的多模态基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为超越纯文本保险欺诈检测提供了一个可复现的合成多模态基准，但其有效性仍需在真实场景、端到端评估和更严格泛化测试中进一步验证。

Abstract: Insurance fraud imposes substantial financial losses and operational inefficiencies, raising premiums and impacting trust among legitimate policyholders. Early detection at FNOL remains a persistent challenge. Existing approaches rely largely on private, text-only datasets, limiting progress on multimodal methods that integrate linguistic, behavioural, and speaker-based indicators. We introduce a synthetic multimodal framework that replicates FNOL conditions. It generates agent-customer dialogue transcripts and two-speaker audios, performs ASR and diarisation. Downstream modules combine NER, regex-based feature extraction, LLM-RAG retrieval, and speaker embeddings in a rule-based risk score to flag narrative reuse, structural inconsistencies, and cross-case voice repetition while balancing sensitivity and false positives. Dataset validation and component-level evaluations show stability and transfer potential, offering a reproducible baseline beyond text-only fraud detection.

</details>


### [124] [The Signal-Coverage Matrix: Stratifying Type and Semantic Errors in Statement Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28013)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Zhanhui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用“信号覆盖矩阵”替代单一的类型正确率（TC%）来评估 LLM 自动形式化，将 Lean elaborator 的通过/失败与语义等价判断交叉，区分真实成功、仅类型正确、仅语义正确和双失败四类错误与改进来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近两年 LLM 自动形式化的 headline TC% 从约 53% 提升到约 76%，但单一指标无法解释不同方法究竟修复了类型错误、语义错误，还是仅提升了表面可通过性，因此需要更细粒度的诊断框架。

Method: 作者构建 signal-coverage matrix，将输出分为 TS、TO、SO、BF 四格，并在 ProofNet# 与 MiniF2F-test 上使用 DeepSeek V4-Pro 比较 Vanilla、Lean-Retry、Sample-Filter、SAF 等方法，分析类型反馈方法带来的跨格迁移、TO-to-TS 恢复率、语义判断器与符号判断器差异等。

Result: 三种 elaborator-feedback 方法带来 +34 到 +36 的 TS 增益，其中约 64% 来自类型层恢复；SO 总体基本持平，原有语义错误中 87.5% 被修复但新增 8 个语义错误。TO-to-TS 比率为 23/61，且能较好预测保留方法的 ΔTS；ΔTC 与 Vanilla elaboration-fail rate 呈强线性关系（R²=0.96）。两个判断器在反馈方法输出上的分歧达 26–37 个百分点，高于 Vanilla 的 7 个百分点。

Conclusion: TC% 的提升不应被简单视为整体质量提升，而应追踪输出在 TS、TO、SO、BF 四类之间的移动；elaborator-feedback 主要改善类型层失败，但也可能引入或掩盖语义问题。

Abstract: Headline type-correctness (TC\%) of LLM autoformalization has climbed from $\sim$53\% to $\sim$76\% in two years, yet this scalar conceals which errors each method resolves. We propose a signal-coverage matrix that crosses the Lean elaborator (pass/fail) with a semantic-equivalence judgment (equivalent/not), sorting every output into one of four cells: true success (TS), type-only (TO), semantic-only (SO), or both fail (BF). On ProofNet\# and MiniF2F-test with DeepSeek V4-Pro across Vanilla, Lean-Retry, Sample-Filter, and Stratified Autoformalization (SAF): (1) the +34 to +36 TS gain across the three elab-feedback methods is $\sim$64\% type-stratum recovery, with SO flat on net (87.5\% of original semantic errors rescued, 8 newly created). (2) The TO-to-TS rate is 23/61 for each method (Wilson 95\% CI [26.6\%, 50.3\%]), and this stratum-level recovery rate predicts $Δ$TS on held-out methods to within 2/186 and renders $Δ$TC linear in the Vanilla elab-fail rate across six (model, dataset) cells ($R^2=0.96$). (3) The two judges disagree by 26 to 37 pp on elab-feedback outputs (vs. 7 pp on Vanilla), with 30 to 56\% of symbolic-judge false negatives traceable to elaborator-forced rewrites. The persistent residual reduces to two gold-formalization errors. TC\% gains should be credited by which cell moved, not by the scalar alone.

</details>


### [125] [A Tree-of-Thoughts Inspired Hybrid Approach for Legal Case Judgement Summarization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28044)
*Aniket Deroy,Kripabandhu Ghosh,Saptarshi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种受 Tree-of-Thoughts 启发的抽取-生成式混合法律判决摘要方法，并在 DeepSeek 与 LLaMA 上验证其优于单纯抽取式或生成式提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律判决摘要研究多集中于传统抽取式或生成式方法，对结合抽取与生成优势的混合式方法探索不足；法律判决文本通常结构复杂、篇幅较长，需要更稳定且信息覆盖更充分的摘要策略。

Method: 作者设计了一种 Tree-of-Thoughts 启发的抽取-生成式摘要提示流程，先利用抽取式思路识别判决中的关键内容，再通过生成式方式组织和改写摘要；实验使用 DeepSeek 和 LLaMA 两类大语言模型，对比抽取式、生成式以及抽取-生成式三种提示策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的抽取-生成式提示在法律判决摘要任务中生成的摘要质量优于其他类型的 LLM 提示方法。

Conclusion: 混合式抽取-生成摘要策略能够更好地利用 LLM 的信息筛选与语言生成能力，适合法律判决摘要场景，并显示出相较单一摘要范式的优势。

Abstract: In recent times, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for legal case judgement summarization. Most prior works have tried traditional extractive and abstractive summarization of case judgements. However, hybrid or extractive-abstractive techniques have not been explored much. In this work, we propose a novel tree-of-thoughts inspired extractive-abstractive summarization approach for legal judgement summarization. We conduct experiments using two popular LLMs, DeepSeek and LLama, and compare among extractive, abstractive and extractive-abstractive summarization. Our experiments show that the proposed extractive-abstractive prompt provides better summaries compared to other types of LLM prompts.

</details>


### [126] [Can LLMs Judge Better Than They Generate? Evaluating Task Asymmetry, Mechanistic Interpretability and Transferability for In-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28050)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在受控的上下文问答场景中检验“评估比生成更容易”这一假设，发现自评并不总是优于生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge 与自我评估流程通常默认模型判断答案正确性比生成答案更容易，但开放域设置会受到模型参数知识干扰。论文希望在仅依赖给定上下文的问答任务中重新验证该假设。

Method: 作者在 SQuAD 2.0、DROP、HotpotQA、MuSiQue 四个基准和两个模型上进行受控实验，让模型先基于上下文生成答案，再判断自己生成答案的正确性；同时进行注意力分析，并用 LoRA 微调分别考察生成与评估能力的迁移和干扰。

Result: 在四个数据集中的三个上，生成准确率高于自我评估，只有多跳 MuSiQue 例外。注意力分析显示，评估阶段对上下文的关注仅为生成阶段的约 1/3 到 1/5，且几乎不充分阅读候选答案。LoRA 微调显示，生成微调会导致过度接受答案，评估微调会损害生成能力。

Conclusion: 评估并不天然比生成更容易，自评流程中的核心假设需要重新审视；生成与评估之间存在显著能力不对称，且不能简单通过任务微调消除。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge and self-evaluation pipelines implicitly assume that evaluation is easier than generation. We test this in a controlled in-context QA setting where a context passage is the sole information source and each model judges the answer it generated, removing the parametric-knowledge confound of open-domain comparisons. Across four benchmarks (SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue) and two models, evaluation is not uniformly easier: generation accuracy exceeds self-evaluation on three of four, with multi-hop MuSiQue the exception. Attention analysis reveals why: evaluation attends to context 3--5x less than generation does and barely reads the candidate answer. LoRA fine-tuning confirms the asymmetry is not a training artifact: generation fine-tuning induces over-acceptance and evaluation fine-tuning degrades generation. These findings challenge core assumptions in self-evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [MultiHashFormer: Hash-based Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28057)
*Huiyin Xue,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiHashFormer 用多个独立哈希函数为每个词元生成唯一的短哈希签名，并通过哈希编解码器实现可用于因果语言模型的哈希式自回归，从而在固定/更小词表参数开销下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型的嵌入矩阵参数量随词表大小线性增长；既有哈希嵌入方法虽能节省参数，但多词元映射到同一向量的多对一碰撞使其难以直接用于需要明确生成下一个词元的因果语言模型。

Method: 提出 MultiHashFormer：用多组独立哈希函数把每个词元编码为唯一哈希签名；Hash Encoder 将离散哈希 ID 序列压缩为单个潜在向量供 Transformer decoder 处理；Hash Decoder 自回归生成下一词元的哈希签名，再映射回文本。

Result: 在 100M、1B、3B 参数规模上进行评估，MultiHashFormer 在多个基准测试中持续优于标准 Transformer 语言模型；同时在多语言词表扩展场景中可保持常数级参数占用且无需结构修改。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过唯一哈希签名与哈希编解码机制，可以克服传统哈希嵌入在因果语言建模中的碰撞限制，实现参数高效、可扩展且性能更强的自回归语言模型。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) represent tokens using embedding matrices that scale linearly with the vocabulary size. To constrain the parameter footprint, prior work proposes hashing many tokens into a single vector within encoder-only models. While this offers parameter efficiency, many-to-one collisions prevent its use in causal LMs. In this paper, we propose MultiHashFormer, a new framework that allows hash-based autoregression. Each token is represented as a unique hash signature, a short sequence of discrete hash IDs, generated by multiple independent hash functions. A Hash Encoder compresses this signature into a single latent vector for processing by a Transformer decoder. Then, a Hash Decoder generates the hash signature of the next token, which is then mapped back to text. We evaluate our approach at the 100M, 1B and 3B parameter scales, demonstrating that MultiHashFormer consistently outperforms standard Transformer LMs across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, we show that our model handles multilingual vocabulary expansion with a constant parameter footprint without any modifications.

</details>


### [128] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向大语言模型训练稳定性的机制驱动早期检测方法，可在损失发散前数千步发现异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型训练依赖庞大加速器集群和长时间计算，一旦数值错误或超参数故障导致训练动态失稳，常规指标如损失和梯度范数可能在很长时间内仍表现正常，从而造成高昂浪费。

Method: 作者从关键模块的功能角色和故障最早产生可观测信号的计算位置出发设计内部监控器：针对低精度 Flash Attention，监控 QK 双线性分解的谱熵，尤其关注其一阶项异常；针对 MoE 路由器，则基于专家选择机制设计相关指标。并通过低精度注意力、大学习率及组合故障进行故障注入实验。

Result: 实验表明，这些内部信号能够为不同类型故障提供可区分的异常特征，并且可在损失真正发散前提前数千步触发告警。

Conclusion: 机制驱动的模块级内部监控比仅依赖损失或梯度范数更适合大规模模型训练的早期失稳检测，有助于降低长程训练中的计算浪费和失败成本。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur. After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [129] [From Tokens to States: LLMs as a Special Case of World Models and the Continuous Path Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28127)
*Paul Dubois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为LLM与世界模型并非对立关系，而是处于同一连续谱：LLM可被视为世界模型的退化特例，世界模型是对LLM约束的逐步放宽与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将LLM的“token预测”和世界模型的“现实模拟”二分化，并受LeCun关于通向通用智能需放弃自回归token预测、转向潜空间架构观点的影响。作者旨在纠正这种过度二元的框架，建立二者之间更统一的理论关系。

Method: 论文采用概念性与理论性分析：首先将LLM形式化为特殊世界模型，即状态空间为所有token序列、动作仅为追加一个token；其次构建从下一token预测（NTP）到JEPA的连续谱，识别多token预测、未来摘要预测、下一潜变量预测等中间形式，并分析沿谱移动时逐步放宽的约束及其代价。

Result: 分析表明，世界模型不是LLM的替代物，而是严格泛化；当前研究已经覆盖从离散token预测到潜空间预测之间的多个中间站点。同时，越接近一般世界模型，越会失去LLM能够大规模训练的两项关键优势：互联网规模自监督文本数据，以及为离散token预测高度适配的Transformer架构。

Conclusion: 论文结论是，应将LLM与世界模型理解为连续谱上的不同点，而非互斥范式。未来关键开放问题包括：如何从自监督文本过渡到带行动标签的环境数据，以及Transformer是否能有效泛化到连续状态预测，或是否需要新的架构原语。

Abstract: The AI community has framed the relationship between large language models (LLMs) and world models as a dichotomy: LLMs predict tokens; world models simulate reality. Yann LeCun argues in 2022 that reaching general intelligence requires abandoning autoregressive token prediction in favour of latent-space architectures. This framing is unnecessarily binary. Two claims will be defended. First, LLMs are a degenerate special case of world models: the state space is the set of all token sequences, the only action is appending one token, and world models are therefore a strict generalisation of LLMs, not a replacement. Second, there is a natural continuous spectrum from NTP to JEPA, with multi-token prediction, future-summary prediction, and next-latent prediction as intermediate stations already populated by current research. Moving along this spectrum relaxes the LLM constraints one by one. It also progressively surrenders the two practical advantages that make LLMs trainable at scale: internet-scale self-supervised data, and a transformer architecture co-designed for discrete token prediction. Both are examined as open research questions: the data question (the cliff from self-supervised text to instrumented action-labelled environments) and the architecture question (whether the transformer generalises to continuous-state prediction, or whether a new primitive is needed).

</details>


### [130] [Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Xinyue Zeng,Zhuochun Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Epi2Diff，将大型推理模型的推理轨迹结构化为认知 episode 序列，用于更准确且可解释地预测人类题目难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育测评需要可靠的题目难度估计以支持公平性和测验构建；现有方法依赖昂贵人工校准或仅基于题目文本，难以解释题目为何困难以及背后的认知过程。

Method: 作者将难度视为题目引发的问题解决负荷的可观测结果，利用 LRM 推理轨迹，将其分段并归纳为具有认知意义的功能性 problem-solving states/episodes，提取 episode 动态特征，如推理规模、努力分配、状态转移，并与语义题目表示结合进行人类难度预测。

Result: 在四个真实人类难度数据集上，Epi2Diff 稳定优于强基线，包括微调小语言模型、LLM in-context learning 和监督式 LLM adaptation；在 SAT 衍生分类基准上，相比监督式 LLM 微调基线取得 8.1% 平均相对提升。

Conclusion: LRM 推理轨迹中的认知 episode 能作为预测性强、可解释的过程表征，用于人类题目难度建模；困难题目主要表现为更费力、迭代性更强、实现导向更明显的 episode 动态，而不只是回答更长。

Abstract: Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.

</details>


### [131] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言模型在视觉证据与记忆知识冲突时的因果机制，发现少量后半层注意力头驱动“先验知识 grounding”，而视觉 grounding 是默认行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要从行为层面描述视觉-语言模型如何处理视觉输入与世界知识冲突，但缺乏组件级、可因果验证的机制解释；理解该冲突解决过程对提升多模态系统可靠性至关重要。

Method: 作者在三个粒度上进行激活修补：残差流、注意力头和 MLP 子层；结合模型组件消融与机制分析，在三个视觉-语言模型家族中定位并验证与先验知识回答相关的关键组件。

Result: 先验知识 grounding 依赖少量因果必要的注意力头，占全部注意力头的 2.5%–4.8%，主要集中在网络后半部分。消融这些头会使 68%–96% 的先验知识提示下预测转向视觉 grounded 答案，但仅改变 0.8%–7.5% 的视觉 grounded 预测。关键头可分为 routing heads 和 writing heads。

Conclusion: 视觉 grounding 是 VLM 的默认路径，而先验知识 grounding 由稀疏且可解释的注意力头电路实现，呈现跨模型家族和规模的一致结构，揭示了感知与知识冲突中的非对称因果机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A Quantum Method of Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27442)
*Arick Grootveld*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出量子版“类型方法”：用经验算子类比经典经验分布，并建立组合计数与大偏差界，应用于复合量子假设检验的通用可达性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论中的类型方法是处理经验分布、组合界和大偏差分析的核心工具，广泛用于复合假设检验、通用信源编码和任意变信道容量等问题；量子信息论中缺少同等通用的量子化工具。

Method: 作者引入一种“经验算子”作为经验分布的量子对应物，并证明其满足与经典类型方法类似的组合界和大偏差界，从而形成量子类型方法框架。

Result: 该经验算子能够支持量子场景下的类型计数与概率衰减分析；基于该框架，作者证明了复合量子假设检验中的通用可达性结果。

Conclusion: 论文建立了经典类型方法的量子类比，为量子信息论中的通用编码、检验和大偏差问题提供了基础性技术工具。

Abstract: The method of types is a fundamental tool in classical information theory, with applications ranging from composite hypothesis testing and universal source coding to the capacity of arbitrarily varying channels. In this work we introduce an empirical operator acting as a quantum analog of the empirical distribution. We show that this empirical operator satisfies combinatorial and large-deviation bounds, which in combination describe a quantum method of types. As an application, we use this quantum method of types to prove a universal achievability result for composite quantum hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [133] [Deriving Approximate Message Passing from the Convex Gaussian Min-Max Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27769)
*Vikrant Malik,Babak Hassibi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了 CGMT 与 AMP/GAMP 在高维正则化回归中的直接联系，说明 CGMT 的辅助优化在特定条件下可恢复 AMP 固定点及 Onsager 修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMP 与 CGMT 都能给出高维估计问题的精确渐近刻画，且常产生相同的标量状态演化方程，但二者之间通常缺乏直接、机制层面的联系。论文旨在解释 CGMT 如何导出 AMP/GAMP 的固定点结构。

Method: 作者研究比例高维 regime 下的正则化线性回归，分析 CGMT 的 Primary Optimization 与 Auxiliary Optimization。当 AO 与 PO 具有相同的原始-对偶解时，比较 CGMT 最优性条件与 AMP 固定点方程，并进一步将 AO 中的高斯向量对应到 AMP 原始通道与残差通道中的高斯扰动；同时扩展到正则化 M-估计以关联标量方差 max-sum GAMP。

Result: 论文证明 CGMT 框架能够恢复 AMP 固定点方程，包括 Onsager correction，并识别出 AO 高斯向量与 AMP/GAMP 通道噪声之间的对应关系。在正则化 M-估计场景下，该观点同样可恢复标量方差 max-sum GAMP 的固定点。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 AMP/GAMP 的迭代形式不仅与 CGMT 的渐近结论一致，而且可由 CGMT 框架直接启发或推导。这为在标准 AMP 推导困难但 CGMT 可用的问题中构造 AMP-like 算法提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: Approximate message passing (AMP) provides fast iterative algorithms for high-dimensional signal recovery with Gaussian design matrices, while the Convex Gaussian Min-max Theorem (CGMT) gives a static optimization framework for obtaining sharp asymptotic characterizations of convex estimators. Although these two frameworks often lead to the same scalar state-evolution equations, their connection is usually indirect. In this paper, we establish a direct connection between the two for regularized linear regression in the proportional high-dimensional regime. When the CGMT Auxiliary Optimization (AO) and Primary Optimization (PO) give the same primal-dual solution, we show that the CGMT framework recovers the AMP fixed-point equations, including the Onsager correction. We further identify the AO Gaussian vectors with the Gaussian perturbations in the primal and residual AMP channels. For regularized M-estimation, the same viewpoint recovers the fixed point of scalar-variance max-sum Generalized AMP (GAMP). These results show that the AMP (and GAMP) iterations are suggested, and can be derived, from the CGMT framework, and may further suggest a way to derive AMP-like algorithms in settings where CGMT applies but standard AMP derivations are unavailable.

</details>


### [134] [Constructions and Characterizations of $s$-Plateaued Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27776)
*Jiaxin Wang,Yadi Wei,Fang-Wei Fu,Jin Li,Fulin Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了 s-plateaued partitions，作为 bent partitions 的推广，用于系统构造多类 plateaued 函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bent partitions 是构造 bent 函数的重要工具，并与编码理论、组合数学密切相关；但现有概念主要对应 0-plateaued 情形，难以覆盖更一般的 s-plateaued 函数构造需求，因此需要建立更广泛的分割理论框架。

Method: 作者定义了向量空间 V_n^(p) 上深度为 K 的 s-plateaued partition，并研究其块 A_i 的可能基数；利用该分割构造 p 元 s-plateaued 函数、向量值 s-plateaued 函数和广义 s-plateaued 函数；进一步在 p 为奇素数、K≥5 且各块满足 -A_i=A_i 的条件下给出刻画。

Result: 论文给出了若干显式构造，这些构造生成的 p 元 s-plateaued 函数没有非零线性结构；证明当 p≥5 且 n+s 为偶数时，满足 f(x)=f(-x) 的 p 元 s-plateaued 函数，其原像集分割是 s-plateaued partition 当且仅当 f 具有 (p−1)-form。对于 s=0 的情形，还部分回应了关于深度 p^(n/2) 的 bent partition 是否必然来自 spreads 的开放问题。

Conclusion: s-plateaued partitions 为 plateaued 函数的统一构造提供了新框架，并揭示了其比 bent partitions 更复杂的结构特征；相关刻画和构造结果扩展了 bent/plateaued 函数理论，并对已有开放问题给出部分进展。

Abstract: Bent partitions play a significant role in constructing bent functions and have rich connections with coding theory and combinatorics. In this paper, we introduce $s$-plateaued partitions, which generalize the bent partitions. Let $Γ=\{A_{i}, 1 \leq i \leq K\}$ be a partition of $V_{n}^{(p)}$, where $V_{n}^{(p)}$ is an $n$-dimensional vector space over the prime field $\mathbb{F}_{p}$ and $p \mid K$. Then $Γ$ is called an $s$-plateaued partition of $V_{n}^{(p)}$ of depth $K$ if each $p$-ary function $f: V_{n}^{(p)} \rightarrow \mathbb{F}_{p}$ for which every $j \in \mathbb{F}_{p}$ has exactly $\frac{K}{p}$ of sets $A_{i}$ in $Γ$ in its preimage set, is a $p$-ary $s$-plateaued function. By using an $s$-plateaued partition, a large number of $p$-ary $s$-plateaued functions, vectorial $s$-plateaued functions and generalized $s$-plateaued functions can be constructed. In particular, $0$-plateaued partitions are just bent partitions. In general, $s$-plateaued partitions are much more complicated than bent partitions. We analyze the possible cardinality of $A_{i}$ of an $s$-plateaued partition. We give some explicit constructions of $s$-plateaued partitions for which any generated $p$-ary $s$-plateaued function has no nonzero linear structure. We give a characterization of an $s$-plateaued partition $Γ=\{A_{i}, 1 \leq i \leq K\}$, where $p$ is odd, $K \geq 5$ and $-A_{i}=A_{i}, 1 \leq i \leq K$. Based on which, we show that if $p \geq 5$, then the preimage set partition of a $p$-ary $s$-plateaued function $f: V_{n}^{(p)} \rightarrow \mathbb{F}_{p}$ with $f(x)=f(-x)$ is an $s$-plateaued partition if and only if $f$ is of $(p-1)$-form, where $n+s$ is even.When $s=0$, we partially address an open problem on whether a bent partition $Γ$ of $V_{n}^{(p)}$ of depth $p^{\frac{n}{2}}$ must be obtained from spreads.

</details>


### [135] [Differential Privacy over Hamming Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27849)
*Borzoo Rassouli,Morteza Varasteh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在二元对称信道上传输计数查询结果时，如何通过优化汉明码码字排列，在不额外加噪、不增加实时计算开销且不降低解码效用的条件下提升差分隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传输信道本身的噪声可带来一定“免费”的隐私保护，但也会导致解码错误。作者关注能否在不进行实时数据混淆、且不增加端到端错误概率的前提下，进一步增强隐私。

Method: 以汉明码作为信道编码器，分析计数查询输出经二元对称信道传输时的隐私-效用关系，并推导一种最优的码字排列方式，使相邻查询输出映射到更有利于差分隐私的码字结构。

Result: 所提出的最优码字排列能够严格改善差分隐私保证，同时不增加实时计算负担，也不造成端到端错误概率上升。

Conclusion: 通过离线优化编码映射/码字排列，可以在保持通信可靠性与系统效用不变的情况下增强隐私保护，说明编码设计本身可作为隐私增强机制。

Abstract: We consider the transmission of the outputs of counting queries over a binary symmetric channel (BSC), where Hamming codes are employed as the channel encoder. Since the channel is inherently noisy, this transmission already provides a degree of privacy protection ``for free'', albeit at the cost of reduced utility in the form of decoding errors. A natural question is whether this privacy can be further improved (i) without any additional real-time obfuscation of the data, such as injecting artificial noise prior to transmission, and (ii) without increasing the end-to-end error probability. In this work, we answer this question in the affirmative by deriving an optimal codeword arrangement that strictly improves differential privacy guarantees while incurring no real-time computational overhead and no degradation in utility.

</details>


### [136] [Performance Analysis and Optimal Design of ORB-Type GRAND Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28030)
*Li Wan,Wenyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向 ORB-type GRAND 的统一分析框架，并基于平均猜测后验（AGP）设计离线重排序算法 RS-ORBGRAND，在 BCH(127,113) 上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRAND 类译码通过按顺序猜测信道错误模式进行译码；ORBGRAND 仅依赖软输出可靠性排序，便于实现。论文希望推广这一思想，建立适用于所有仅依赖可靠性排名的 ORB-type GRAND 的统一性能分析与优化准则。

Method: 引入平均猜测后验（AGP）作为衡量错误模式有效性的核心量，将译码测试顺序转化为错误模式空间上的排序问题。针对随机码集推导固定测试预算下的 BLER、停止时间分布和平均测试次数精确表达式，并分解目标漏检与目标抢占错误。针对固定线性分组码，进一步分离码相关的目标抢占项，并用码字元组的高阶重量关系刻画，同时给出可计算的一阶上界。基于 AGP 提出离线重洗牌方案 RS-ORBGRAND。

Result: 理论上证明在给定错误模式集合内，按 AGP 非递增顺序测试是最优的。实验中，RS-ORBGRAND 在 BCH(127,113) 码上持续优于现有 ORB-type GRAND 算法，并在 BLER 为 10^-6 时距离最大似然译码下界基准仅约 0.1 dB。

Conclusion: AGP 提供了统一理解和优化 ORB-type GRAND 的有效工具；RS-ORBGRAND 通过离线 AGP 重排序能在保持实现友好性的同时接近最大似然译码性能。

Abstract: Guessing Random Additive Noise Decoding (GRAND) performs decoding by sequentially guessing channel error patterns (EPs). Ordered Reliability Bits GRAND (ORBGRAND) is a notable instance suitable for efficient implementation, as it schedules EPs solely according to the ranking of soft channel outputs. In this paper, we generalize this principle to a broader class of GRAND algorithms whose testing order depends only on reliability ranking, referred to as ORB-type GRAND. We develop a unified analytical framework based on a key quantity termed the average guessing posterior (AGP), which captures the effectiveness of each EP and reduces decoding into an ordering problem over the EP space. For random code ensembles, we derive exact expressions for the block error rate (BLER), stopping-time distribution, and average number of tests under a fixed test budget. The analysis separates target-miss and target-preemption errors and shows that ordering EPs by non-increasing AGP is optimal over the EP set under consideration. For fixed linear block codes, we derive the BLER expression that isolates the code-dependent target-preemption term and characterize this term through higher-order weight relationships of codeword tuples, with a computable first-order upper bound as a useful special case. Guided by these insights, we formulate ReShuffled-ORBGRAND (RS-ORBGRAND) as an offline AGP-based reshuffling scheme. Numerical results for the Bose--Chaudhuri--Hocquenghem (BCH)$(127,113)$ code show that RS-ORBGRAND consistently improves existing ORB-type GRAND algorithms and lies within $0.1$~dB of a maximum-likelihood decoding lower-bound benchmark at a BLER of $10^{-6}$.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 World Wide AI-Model Network（AI-ModelNet）概念，旨在通过模型互联、能力共享与协同推理，解决大模型轻量化、私有化、领域化发展后异构模型之间难以有效协作的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练成本高、部署复杂，实际应用正转向轻量、私有和领域专用模型；但异构模型快速增多且分布广泛，模型间交互与协作成为制约大模型生态发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 作者借鉴互联网的发展逻辑，提出 AI-ModelNet 的概念、愿景与系统架构；回顾单模型与多模型研究现状，设计分层体系结构，并通过原型系统和多类应用案例验证框架可行性。

Result: 论文展示了 AI-ModelNet 可支持模型之间建立连接通路，实现能力共享、模型互联和协同推理；原型系统与应用案例表明该范式具有一定可行性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet 被定位为面向未来异构模型生态的新型网络化协作范式，有望提升模型资源复用与协同智能能力；后续仍需在标准协议、调度机制、安全隐私、可信协作和规模化部署等方面继续研究。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [138] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究人格提示是否会影响多智能体大语言模型团队的客观任务表现，发现影响强烈依赖任务结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，人格提示会显著改变大语言模型的沟通风格，例如低宜人性导致更具对抗性的语言，高宜人性导致更合作的表达；但这种沟通风格变化是否会进一步影响实际任务结果，尤其是在多个任务领域中的系统性影响，仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 作者在前沿大语言模型构成的多智能体团队中操纵人格特质，重点考察宜人性差异，并在三个任务域中评估团队表现：结构化编程任务、开放式研究协作任务和竞争性议价任务。通过比较不同人格组合下的沟通行为与任务完成效果，分析人格组成对团队绩效的作用。

Result: 实验发现，人格效应高度依赖任务结构。在结构化编程任务中，低宜人性会显著改变沟通风格，但对里程碑完成影响较小；而在开放式协作和议价任务中，相同的人格操纵会显著降低任务表现。

Conclusion: 人格提示不仅影响模型的交流方式，其对任务绩效的影响取决于任务本身的结构化程度。对于多智能体系统设计而言，简单地通过人格操纵塑造沟通风格存在局限，需要根据任务类型审慎配置智能体人格特征。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [139] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种让LLM智能体具备可校准前瞻规划能力的三阶段训练范式，用文本化未来状态展开与成功估计来模拟内部世界模型，并在搜索与数学推理任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长程序列决策中多为反应式，缺乏类似人类“what-if”推演的内部世界模型，难以在执行前评估候选计划的未来结果。直接用前瞻轨迹微调又容易产生形式模仿而非真实预测能力，即存在“格式-能力鸿沟”。

Method: 作者训练单一自回归模型同时生成计划条件下的未来状态展开和类似Q值的文本化成功估计。训练流程分三阶段：WM-AMT用于在策略中注入潜在预测能力；FE-SFT用于将该能力组织成可用的前瞻输出格式；FC-RL用于强化生成模拟的校准性与决策效用。

Result: 在搜索任务和数学推理任务上的评估显示，该方法相较其他训练基线持续取得更好表现，说明其前瞻模拟与成功估计能更有效支持长程决策。

Conclusion: 有效的LLM智能体内部世界建模不能仅依赖后训练阶段的格式化前瞻样本，而需要先构建预测能力、再诱导格式、最后通过强化学习校准与优化的能力优先训练管线。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [140] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ODYSSEY 提出一个基于范畴论与层论的框架，用于将可验证、局部保真的基础模型构造成多个“foundry”组件的组合，并通过 UFL、FSQL 与 TICKET 支持构建、认证、查询和诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在跨领域知识整合、局部语境保持、可验证性、可追溯更新以及人类可解释视图方面存在不足；论文希望用统一的数学框架管理异质知识构件及其论证、约束和组合规则。

Method: 提出 ODYSSEY 框架，将 foundry 定义为带有论证组件的组织化知识层；用 Universal Foundry Learning 将构建形式化为左右 Kan 扩张的组合；设计 Foundry SQL 作为类型化查询接口；使用 TICKET 认证外部或预构建模型进入持久状态，并实现多类通用与具体 foundry。

Result: 系统已实现并在多种 concrete foundries 上测试，展示同一套范畴机制可支持领域构建、artifact replay、层诊断、Toulmin/局部 LLM 审查、残余障碍台账以及兼容 TICKET 的因果声明抽取。

Conclusion: 该摘要主张 ODYSSEY 能作为一种统一、可验证且可组合的基础模型构建框架，但摘要缺少量化实验、基准比较和失败案例，更多呈现为理论与系统教程型工作。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [141] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DysLexLens：一个面向低资源在线论坛语境的 LLM 框架，用于分析读写障碍学习者使用 AI 工具的经验，并强调可追溯证据、知识图谱推理与定量/定性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 读写障碍学习者越来越多地使用 AI 支持阅读、写作、组织与学习任务，但其真实使用体验尚缺乏系统研究；同时，相关在线论坛数据噪声高、资源稀缺，亟需一种可复现、可验证的分析框架。

Method: DysLexLens 采用端到端架构：先用词典驱动过滤方法从 Reddit 中构建聚焦于读写障碍与 AI 的语料；再结合 LLM 辅助语义分析与知识图谱问答推理；随后生成带证据支撑的查询响应；最后使用 RAGAS、Query Robustness 等定量指标及关注幻觉和证据一致性的人工质性评估指南进行验证。

Result: 作者使用与读写障碍相关的 Reddit 数据和 30 个问题进行演示，结果表明该框架能够从低资源论坛语料中提取有意义模式，并具备推广到其他低资源论坛数据场景的潜力。代码、样例数据、问题和评估结果在 GitHub 开放以支持复现。

Conclusion: DysLexLens 为研究特定学习者群体的 AI 使用体验提供了一个低资源、证据可追溯、结合知识图谱与 LLM 的可复现分析方案，并通过多层评估机制增强了生成结果的可信度。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [142] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现显式推理虽提升可解释性，但不必然提升多模态情感识别准确率；提出MER-R1通过强化学习融合“快思考”的高召回与“慢思考”的高精度，取得SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的多模态大语言模型常假设更充分的显式推理能带来更好MER性能，但作者观察到直接回答的快思考有时优于经过 deliberation 的慢思考。因此，需要解释快慢思考在MER中的差异，并设计机制使推理真正提升识别效果。

Method: 作者首先进行经验分析，比较快思考与慢思考在召回率、精确率和置信度分布上的差异；随后提出MER-R1强化学习框架。该框架包含双目标解耦，将召回与精度拆分为不同优化信号并联合优化；同时引入慢—快置信度校准，使最终慢思考答案与快思考直觉对齐，强化正确情感、抑制错误类别。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion上的大量实验表明，MER-R1达到当前最优性能。实验还显示，快思考倾向于更宽泛、更自信的预测并提升召回，慢思考倾向于保守筛除错误类别并提升精度。

Conclusion: 显式推理本身并不保证MER性能提升，关键在于协调快思考的召回优势与慢思考的精度优势。MER-R1通过强化学习、目标解耦和置信度校准有效缓解二者权衡，并从理论上说明其可降低优化中的方差干扰，使推理真正服务于多模态情感识别。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [143] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tree of Evidence（ToE），用于自动事实核查，通过层级证据推理与多源检索提升对普通及对抗污染输入的验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假信息快速传播，尤其是 AI 生成的误导内容可通过 GEO poisoning 被检索系统系统性呈现，进而污染大语言模型推理；因此需要更鲁棒、可解释的自动事实核查方法。

Method: 将待验证声明建模为动态扩展的论证树；结合强化学习驱动的多源检索代理、证据评估代理和论证树聚合算法，迭代地分解声明、检索证据并完成验证；同时给出检索过程的理论分析和误差界。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同骨干 LLM 上，ToE 相比竞争基线提升 4 到 24 个百分点；在对抗性污染输入上提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: ToE 通过层级化、可解释的证据链推理增强了自动事实核查能力，并在理论上为检索策略收敛到信息论最优策略邻域提供保证。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由符号反馈驱动的迭代自我优化框架，用于提升大语言模型在长时域规划任务中的可行性、正确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在规划任务中受到广泛关注，但在复杂、长时域决策场景下容易生成不可行或错误方案，暴露出鲁棒性与可靠性不足的问题。因此，需要一种能够显式利用任务约束、发现错误并指导模型修正的机制。

Method: 论文提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自我精炼框架：首先通过自然语言提示机制将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述，帮助LLM理解任务语义与约束；其次设计符号验证器检测规划错误，并将错误转化为LLM可理解的纠正指令；同时引入计划识别器推断目标可达性，从而为后续自我修正提供更有效的目标导向反馈。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在长时域规划任务中能够持续提升生成计划的可行性与正确性，优于缺乏符号反馈或验证机制的规划方式。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将符号验证、自然语言反馈与LLM自我精炼结合，可以有效增强LLM规划系统的鲁棒性和可靠性，并为构建更可信的AI规划系统提供了有价值的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [145] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究图世界模型在长时域规划中的滚动误差，提出统一的固定边/动态边框架、误差上界分析以及 Error-Aware GWM，以缓解长时域误差发散并降低规划遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多规划环境天然表示为由智能体、工具、技能、路线和依赖关系构成的图，而非向量或图像。图结构中局部预测误差可能被拓扑放大并沿边传播；当边本身也需要预测时，误差机制更复杂。因此需要系统分析 Graph World Models 在长时域 rollout 中的误差来源与传播规律。

Method: 作者构建了统一的固定边与动态边 GWM 框架，并引入动作节点以支持节点级、边级和图级决策；提出图值 rollout 误差界，将拓扑诱导放大与模型诱导放大分离；针对动态边 rollout 设计联合节点-边算子；基于理论分析提出 Error-Aware GWM，结合谱正则化、rollout 一致性约束和关键节点加权。
